We propose CobSeg, a novel multi-branch architecture that separates coherence-level semantic continuity from lexical boundary transitions and recovers both through directional boundary prediction. CobSeg further uses boundary informativeness weighting to emphasize high-utility utterance positions, and incorporates a corpus-derived topic coherence cue with learned combination weights. While CobSeg is evaluated as a compact trainable segmenter under supervised gold-boundary training and a pseudo-label setting with automatically induced boundaries, it performs enhanced boundary prediction without LLM calls during inference. Across five benchmarks, it improves $P_k$ and $W_d$ particularly when local lexical cues are prominent: under gold supervision, it reduces $P_k$ by 0.7 points and $W_d$ by 0.6 points on VHF, and reaches $P_k$ of 1.0 on DialSeg711; with induced boundaries, it reduces $P_k$ by 14.8 points on VHF, by 1.5 points on DialSeg711, and by 1.1 points on TIAGE, outperforming prior non-LLM approaches.

</details>


### [78] [TeachObs: A Human-Validated Benchmark for Multimodal Teaching Observation and Model Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30673)
*Yeil Jeong,Youngjin Yoo,Seobin Sohn,Hyejin Han,Jinseo Lee,Scott Howard,Unggi Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TeachObs是一个多模态教学观察基准，包含30个来自八个国家的公开课程视频，分为5158个15秒场景，并由研究人员用39个二元观察代码进行标注，同时提供专家对课程的整体评价，用于评估前沿视觉大模型在课堂视频分析中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的课堂视频虽然包含丰富的教学行为信息，但缺乏结构化、可用于模型评估的标注数据，限制了AI在教学分析中的应用。因此需要构建一个高质量、多模态的教学观察基准。

Method: 构建TeachObs基准，包括30个国际课程视频，切分为5158个15秒场景；七名研究者使用39个二元代码（20个视觉+19个非视觉）进行标注，并基于Krippendorff's alpha构建可靠片段标签；三位专家对课程进行整体评分和定性评估；在此基础上评估五种具备视觉能力的前沿大语言模型在三个任务上的表现：纯文本片段编码、图文片段编码和课程级LLM-as-judge评估。

Result: 五个前沿大模型在三个评估任务中无一始终领先；加入中间帧图像会同时增加正确和错误归因；模型倾向于高估流程清晰课程的质量，与专家评分存在偏差。

Conclusion: TeachObs支持细粒度标注和整节课评估，揭示了AI系统在不同学科、课堂形式和标注难度下辅助教学分析的潜力与局限，表明专家判断在复杂教学评估中仍不可替代。

Abstract: Classroom videos contain observable teaching practices, but their pedagogical and visual signals are rarely organized in forms suitable for model evaluation. We present \textit{TeachObs}, a human-validated benchmark for multimodal teaching observation in classroom videos. \textit{TeachObs} includes 30 public lesson videos from eight countries divided into 5,158 fixed 15-second scenes. Seven researchers annotated each scene with 39 binary observation codes, covering 20 visual codes, such as gesture, board work, pointing, and visual materials, and 19 nonvisual codes, such as instruction, monitoring, questioning, feedback, and reflection. Gold segment labels are constructed using reliability- and prevalence-aware rules based on Krippendorff's alpha. In addition to segment-level labels, three expert raters produced lesson-level ratings and qualitative evaluations of instructional design, instructional delivery, learner response, learning materials, and lesson closure across the 30 lessons, with rater coverage detailed in the body. Using these two human reference layers, we evaluate five vision-capable frontier LLMs across three tracks - text-only segment coding, text + frame segment coding, and lesson-level coverage scored under an LLM-as-judge protocol - and find that no single model consistently outperforms others across all three tracks, that adding a mid-frame inflates both true and false attributions per scene, and that model evaluations over-rate procedurally clear lessons relative to expert raters. \textit{TeachObs} therefore supports both fine-grained annotation benchmarking and whole-lesson evaluation, showing where AI systems can assist classroom video analysis and where expert judgment remains necessary across varied subjects, classroom formats, and annotation difficulty levels.

</details>


### [79] [Human-Alignment, Calibration, and Activation Patterns in Large Language Model Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30675)
*Kyle Moore,Jesse Roberts,Daryl Watson,William Ward,Grayson Heyboer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了大语言模型中的不确定性与人类不确定性的相似性，提出了“不确定性对齐”的概念，并分析了其在多种数据集上的表现以及指令微调对其影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型的行为分析中，不确定性量化主要关注校准性以应对幻觉问题，但缺乏对模型不确定性是否与人类相似的研究。本文旨在填补这一空白，探索模型与人类不确定性之间的对齐程度。

Method: 通过分析大语言模型的显性行为和内部激活模式，评估其在多项选择和开放式事实回忆任务中的不确定性对齐和校准性，并考察指令微调对这些特性的影响。

Result: 研究发现，大语言模型在不同数据集上表现出一定程度的人类相似不确定性信号，且部分情况下同时具备良好的对齐性和校准性；指令微调显著影响这两种特性的表现。

Conclusion: 大语言模型中存在与人类不确定性相似的信号，不确定性对齐可作为除校准性外的新评估维度，指令微调在塑造该特性中起关键作用。

Abstract: Uncertainty Quantification is a large and growing subfield of large language model behavioral analysis. Primarily to recognize and combat hallucination, the field has largely focused on measuring and improving calibration, the accuracy of uncertainty judgments to task efficacy. In this work, we investigate the relatively underexplored question of how similar large language model uncertainty is to human uncertainty. We investigate the presence and strength of human-similar uncertainty signals, deemed uncertainty alignment, in large language model overt behavior and internal activation patterns. We identify whether the models show evidence of simultaneous alignment and calibration on a variety of datasets covering both multiple choice and open ended factual recall. And we characterize the effect of instruct fine-tuning on each of these facets.

</details>


### [80] [ElasticMem: Latent Memory as a Learnable Resource for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30690)
*Tao Feng,Chongrui Ye,Tianyang Luo,Jingjun Xu,Xueqiang Xu,Haozhen Zhang,Ge Liu,Jiaxuan You*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ElasticMem是一种用于大语言模型的弹性记忆增强框架，通过学习将记忆作为弹性的潜在资源来优化长期记忆使用，在多个任务中显著提升了性能并降低了令牌开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆增强方法通常将记忆视为固定资源，导致查询依赖的记忆效用与固定分配之间存在不匹配问题。

Method: ElasticMem构建了一个离线潜在记忆库，从推理器隐藏状态自适应检索记忆，并通过学习策略为每个检索到的记忆分配可变的潜在预算，最后将选定的潜在状态作为软记忆令牌注入生成过程，整个记忆使用过程通过下游任务奖励进行优化。

Result: 在MemorySuite上的评估显示，ElasticMem在Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct和Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct模型上分别将加权平均QA准确率提高了26.2%和24.6%，并将ALFWorld任务成功率提高了66.3%和27.2%，同时实现了最低的令牌成本。

Conclusion: 自适应检索和弹性预算分配使ElasticMem能够优先选择有用证据和可迁移计划，优于传统的基于余弦相似度的刚性方法。

Abstract: Long-term memory is essential for LLM agents to reason coherently across extended interactions, personalize responses, and reuse past experience. However, existing memory-augmented methods typically treat memory as a fixed resource: text-space approaches concatenate retrieved memories into the context window, causing substantial token overhead and sensitivity to noisy evidence, while latent-space approaches reduce textual cost but still rely on rigid retrieval or fixed-capacity memory interfaces. This creates a mismatch between query-dependent memory utility and fixed memory allocation. We propose ElasticMem, a memory-augmented LLM framework that learns to use memory as an elastic latent resource. ElasticMem builds an offline latent memory bank with retrieval keys and content caches, retrieves memories adaptively from the reasoner's hidden state, assigns each retrieved memory a variable latent budget through a learned policy, and injects selected latent states as soft memory tokens for generation. The full memory-use process is optimized with downstream task rewards through group-relative policy optimization. We evaluate ElasticMem on MemorySuite, covering memory-intensive QA and embodied agent control. Across Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct and Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct backbones, ElasticMem improves weighted average QA accuracy by 26.2% and 24.6%, and improves ALFWorld success rate by 66.3% and 27.2%, respectively, over the strongest baselines, while achieving the lowest ALFWorld token cost. Ablations and qualitative analyses further show that adaptive retrieval and elastic budget allocation help ElasticMem prioritize useful evidence and transferable plans beyond rigid cosine similarity. Our code for ElasticMem will be released at https://github.com/ulab-uiuc/ElasticMem.

</details>


### [81] [Neuron-Level Interventions for Gendered and Gender-Neutral Generation in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30717)
*Zhiwen You,Nafiseh Nikeghbal,Jana Diesner*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了语言模型中性别相关神经元的分布，提出了一种神经元级别的干预方法，能够更精确地控制生成文本中的性别表达，减少非目标性别的泄露，并在两个开源语言模型上验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多从二元性别视角分析语言模型中的性别偏见，忽视了性别中立形式，且对性别信息如何在模型内部表征缺乏深入理解。

Method: 提出一种基于神经元级别干预的方法，识别与女性、男性和性别中立三类相关的神经元，并通过受控生成实验验证其对性别倾向的调控能力；构建包含三类性别标签的受控句子数据集，并通过人工评估确保数据质量。

Result: 发现性别相关神经元主要集中于模型的早期层，后期层贡献较小；所提方法在性别控制精度、减少性别泄露和生成质量方面优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了语言模型中性别信息的编码机制，提供了一种简单而有效的性别干预手段，有助于神经元分析和性别偏见缓解。

Abstract: Language models (LMs) can produce gendered language and stereotypes even when given neutral prompts. Most prior work on gender bias in LMs primarily examines gender through a binary lens (feminine vs. masculine), with limited attention to gender-neutral forms, such as they/them pronouns or neutrally phrased job titles. How gender-related signals are encoded in the internal representations of LMs remains an open question. In this work, we study gender-specific neurons in LMs across three categories: feminine, masculine, and gender-neutral. We propose a neuron-level intervention method to identify neurons that are strongly tied to each gender category. We then test these neurons through controlled generation, showing that activating or masking gender-related neurons can steer a sentence toward a target gender form while preserving its original meaning. To evaluate the effectiveness of our gender-intervention approach, we curate two datasets with controlled sentences labeled across all three gender categories and validate the data quality through human evaluation. Experiments on two open-source LMs show that gender-specific neurons are not evenly distributed across model layers; instead, they concentrate heavily in the earliest layers with smaller contributions from later layers. Compared to existing methods, our method achieves more precise gender control, with less leakage into non-target gender categories and stable output quality through two evaluation criteria. Overall, our work examines how gender is encoded in LMs and provides a simple yet effective approach toward controlled gender intervention for both neuron intervention evaluation and gender bias mitigation. Code and datasets are available at: https://github.com/zhiwenyou103/Gender-Neuron-Intervention

</details>


### [82] [Unlocking Fine-Grained Translation Quality Estimation in LRMs through Synergistically Evolving Implicit and Explicit Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31378)
*Renfei Dang,Xinye Wang,Zhejian Lai,Weilu Xu,Shimin Tao,Daimeng Wei,Min Zhang,Shujian Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为RIEQE的双阶段训练框架，通过隐式和显式推理能力的协同进化来提升大型推理模型在细粒度翻译质量估计（QE）任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大型推理模型具备强大的多语言能力，但在细粒度翻译质量估计任务上仍表现不佳，主要挑战在于任务本身的复杂性而非模型能力不足。

Method: 提出RIEQE框架，包含两个阶段：第一阶段进行无推理链的监督微调（NonThinking-SFT），增强模型的隐式推理能力；第二阶段采用可验证奖励的强化学习（Thinking-RLVR），加强显式推理能力。

Result: 在WMT测试集上，基于Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507的RIEQE模型在显式推理性能上超越所有基线模型，其隐式推理能力也与当前最优的编码器模型相当。

Conclusion: 隐式与显式推理在所提框架下能够协同进化并相互促进，有效提升模型在细粒度QE任务上的整体性能。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) still struggle with fine-grained translation quality estimation (QE), even with long reasoning chains. We argue that LRMs already possess strong multilingual capabilities, while the core challenge stems from the intrinsic difficulty of learning the fine-grained QE task. In this paper, we propose RIEQE (Reasoning both Implicitly and Explicitly for QE), a simple two-stage training framework that enables the co-evolution of implicit (layer-wise) and explicit (token-wise) reasoning capabilities. To make implicit reasoning feasible, we first decompose the complex QE task into straightforward subtasks. Based on this, our two-stage approach applies: (1) NonThinking-SFT, Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) without reasoning chains to directly boost the model's implicit reasoning tendency and capability; and (2) Thinking-RLVR, standard Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Reward (RLVR) to subsequently strengthen explicit reasoning. Results demonstrate that implicit and explicit reasoning synergistically co-evolve under our framework. On the WMT test sets, RIEQE based on Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507 surpasses all baselines in explicit reasoning performance, while its implicit reasoning capability is also comparable to the best current encoder-based models. We further provide evidence for the synergistic collaboration between implicit and explicit reasoning, showing how they mutually benefit each other.

</details>


### [83] [LLM Judges Inconsistently Disagree Across Safety Criteria and Harm Categories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31381)
*Krishnapriya Vishnubhotla,Soumya Vajjala,Akriti Vij,Isar Nejadgholi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 大型语言模型在无参考设置下进行多维度安全评估时表现出不一致性，尤其在金融等受监管领域提供的机器生成建议的安全问题识别上不可靠，但在明显有害内容（如暴力）的识别上较可靠。不同评判者之间存在高度分歧，且判断的一致性受安全标准、内容语言和语言风格影响。研究为如何在实际场景中使用自动化评判者提供了建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估大型语言模型作为自动化评判者在多维度安全评价中的可靠性，特别是在受监管领域中对机器生成建议进行安全评估的一致性问题。

Method: 在无参考设置下，通过多维度安全评估框架测试多个大型语言模型作为评判者的判断一致性，分析其在不同领域、安全标准和语言风格下的表现差异。

Result: 发现大型语言模型在识别金融等领域建议的安全问题时不可靠，但在识别明显有害内容（如暴力）时更可靠；判断结果受安全标准、语言和语言风格显著影响；不同评判者间存在高度不一致。

Conclusion: 当前大型语言模型作为自动化评判者存在显著局限性，需谨慎使用，尤其是在高风险领域。研究建议应结合多种评判机制并考虑语境因素以提升评估可靠性。

Abstract: We evaluate the consistency of automated judges in conducting a multi-dimensional safety evaluation in a reference-free setup. Our results indicate that Large Language Models are unreliable judges in identifying safety issues related to machine-generated advice in regulated domains such as finance, although they are more reliable at identifying more overt forms of unsafe/harmful content such as violence. The degree of inconsistency in a model's judgments can vary significantly by the chosen safety criteria and can be impacted by the language of the content and its linguistic style as well. Finally, there is high disagreement among different judges for the same output, across domains, safety criteria, and languages. These findings provide new insights on the practice of using LLMs as evaluators and offer several recommendations for practitioners on how to use automated judges in practical scenarios.

</details>


### [84] [Target-Side Paraphrase Augmentation for Sign Language Translation with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31393)
*Pedro Dal Bianco,Jean Paul Nunes Reinhold,Oscar Stanchi,Facundo Quiroga,Franco Ronchetti,Ulisses Brisolara Corrêa*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探索了在手语翻译（SLT）中使用GPT-4o生成目标句的受控释义变体以进行目标端增强的方法，并在三个具有不同挑战的数据集上评估其效果，发现该方法能提升翻译质量，尤其在语义保真度方面，但受限于数据稀疏性和重复性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于配对的手语视频/文本语料库有限且目标词汇分布呈重尾特性，手语翻译性能受限，因此需要通过数据增强来改善模型泛化能力。

Method: 采用GPT-4o生成参考句子的释义变体，保持手语输入不变；使用基于姿态的Signformer-style Transformer模型，先在增强语料上预训练，再在原始数据上微调。

Result: 在PHOENIX14T上BLEU-4从9.56提升至10.33；GSL和LSA-T的结果显示该方法在高度重复或极度稀疏的情况下存在局限；语义评估表明语义保真度提升超过传统指标反映的程度。

Conclusion: 这是首次将大语言模型生成的释义和LLM-as-a-Judge用于手语翻译的研究，目标端增强有助于缓解数据稀缺问题，但在极端情况下效果有限，未来需结合更优的语义评估机制。

Abstract: Sign language translation (SLT) remains constrained by limited paired sign-video/text corpora and heavy-tailed target vocabularies. We study target-side augmentation in which GPT-4o generates controlled paraphrase variants of reference sentences while the sign input remains unchanged. A Signformer-style pose-based Transformer is trained under a two-stage schedule: pre-training on the augmented corpus followed by fine-tuning on the original references.
  We evaluate on three datasets spanning complementary challenges: PHOENIX14T (German Sign Language), with moderate lexical diversity; GSL (Greek Sign Language), with highly ontrolled, repetitive recordings; and LSA-T (Argentinian Sign Language), with severe long-tail sparsity. On PHOENIX14T, augmentation improves BLEU-4 from 9.56 to 10.33. The near-saturated GSL baseline and extremely sparse LSA-T setting reveal the limits of the approach. To our knowledge, this is the first study to apply LLM-generated target-side araphrases and LLM-as-a-Judge evaluation to SLT. The semantic evaluation reveals gains in fidelity that lexical overlap metrics understate.

</details>


### [85] [The Sword, Shield, and Achilles' Heel: Characterizing the Linguistic Inductive Bias of Large Language Models for Spatial Reasoning in Navigation Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31404)
*Xudong Zhang,Jian Yang,Shengkai Wang,Jiangpeng Tian,Shaowen Chen,Xian Wei,Ke Li,Xiong You*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种双干预框架，用于分析大型语言模型（LLM）在基于文本的空间表示中进行导航规划时的语言归纳偏见，揭示了拓扑信息的关键作用、语言格式的双刃剑效应以及语义信息的潜在风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM-based导航系统通常将空间表示转化为文本输入，但忽略了语言结构和上下文特征选择对LLM行为的影响。本文旨在探究这些因素如何塑造LLM的导航规划能力。

Method: 提出了一种双干预框架：表示干预（改变语言格式和压缩程度）和上下文干预（结合上下文特征组合与冲突探测），以分离语言结构与不同上下文线索的影响。

Result: 实验表明：拓扑信息是稳健规划的基础；语言格式的效果取决于模型规模、任务需求和压缩水平；而错误的语义线索会严重破坏规划过程。

Conclusion: 有效的基于文本的空间表示应保持拓扑完整性，根据模型能力调整表示压缩程度，并确保语义正确性，而非采用单一表示方式。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM)-based navigation systems commonly construct explicit spatial representations (e.g., topological graphs, semantic raster maps) and translate them into textual descriptions as LLMs' inputs. However, the linguistic structures of such text-based spatial representations and the choices of contextual features (e.g., topology, geometry) they contain are often treated as neutral engineering decisions rather than key factors that shape LLMs' behavior. To fill the gap, we propose a dual-interventional framework that disentangles linguistic structures from different contextual cues to evaluate the linguistic inductive bias of LLMs for navigation planning. In the framework, representation intervention varies the linguistic format and the degree of linguistic compression, clarifying when linguistic representations support or inhibit navigation planning. Context intervention, combined with contextual feature combination and conflict probing, explicitly clarifies the preferences and weaknesses of LLMs when processing different contextual cues. Experiments across diverse spatial reasoning tasks and multiple model scales reveal a consistent pattern: topological information is a sturdy shield and the backbone of robust planning; linguistic format is a double-edged sword whose effect depends on model size, task demands, and the compression level; and semantic information is a fatal Achilles' heel -- incorrect semantic cues can systematically derail the planning process. Overall, our study shows that effective text-based spatial representations in LLM-based navigation should preserve topological integrity, calibrate representational compression to model capacity, and ensure semantic correctness, rather than simply adopting a single representation. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/jonesdong150/LLM-Navigation-Inductive-Bias.

</details>


### [86] [SCOPE: Self-Play via Co-Evolving Policies for Open-Ended Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31433)
*Wai-Chung Kwan,Aryo Pradipta Gema,Joshua Ong Jun Leang,Pasquale Minervini*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SCOPE是一种无需数据的自对弈框架，用于开放性任务，通过协同进化挑战者和解决者策略，并利用冻结的初始模型作为自评判者，显著提升了语言模型在多个基准上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自对弈方法依赖规则可检查的答案或人工标注数据，难以应用于开放性任务，因此需要一种无需外部监督且能处理开放性任务的新框架。

Method: 提出SCOPE框架，包含两个协同进化的策略：Challenger生成基于文档的任务，Solver通过多轮检索回答；使用冻结的初始模型作为自评判者，生成评分标准并评估回答。

Result: 在三个7-8B模型上，SCOPE在八个基准上最多提升+10.4分，性能匹配或超过使用约9K人工标注提示训练的GRPO_data；在七个未见过的短问答基准上最多提升+13.8分，且全面超越GRPO_data。

Conclusion: SCOPE证明了无需外部数据或人工标注的自对弈可用于开放性任务，通过协同进化保持任务难度贴近模型能力前沿，自评判机制有效推动性能提升，但评分标准生成质量仍是瓶颈。

Abstract: Self-play can train language models without external supervision. However, existing methods require rule-checkable answers, leaving open-ended tasks dependent on curated prompts or frontier-model judges. We introduce SCOPE, a data-free self-play framework for open-ended tasks that co-evolves two policies: a Challenger that generates document-grounded tasks, and a Solver that answers them through multi-turn retrieval. A frozen copy of the initial model serves as the self-judge, which writes task-specific rubrics from the source document and grades Solver responses against them. Across three 7-8B instruction-tuned models (Qwen2.5, Qwen3, OLMo-3), SCOPE improves open-ended performance by up to +10.4 points on eight benchmarks and matches or exceeds GRPO_data trained on ~9K curated prompts. Although trained only on open-ended tasks, SCOPE also improves held-out short-form QA by up to +13.8 points on seven held-out benchmarks, surpassing GRPO_data on all three models. Ablations show that co-evolving the Challenger is necessary to keep tasks near the Solver's frontier, that gains arise from improvements in both retrieval and synthesis with the relative contribution varying by task, and that rubric generation quality is the bottleneck for self-judging.

</details>


### [87] [Fine-grained Verification via Diagnostic Reasoning Supervision for Aspect Sentiment Triplet Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31446)
*Wenna Lai,Haoran Xie,Guandong Xu,Qing Li,S. Joe Qin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种细粒度验证框架FiVeD，用于提升方面情感三元组抽取（ASTE）的可靠性，通过多任务训练和诊断性推理监督，显著提高了多个基线模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ASTE方法缺乏对提取结果的后验验证，导致可能产生全局无效的局部合理预测，影响系统可靠性。此外，错误类型多样且候选结果的质量具有连续性，需要更精细的验证机制。

Method: 提出了FiVeD框架，结合有效性分类和质量评分为主任务，错误类型分类和理由生成为辅助任务；定义了层次化错误类别，构造语义和句法约束下的错误三元组，并利用现成大语言模型根据特定规则生成质量分数和诊断理由。

Result: 在多个ASTE基线上实验表明，作为即插即用模块，FiVeD最多可将F1分数提高3.53点，能有效过滤候选输出并支持灵活的精度-召回权衡。

Conclusion: FiVeD通过细粒度验证和诊断监督显著提升了ASTE系统的可靠性和性能，具备良好的通用性和实用性。

Abstract: Aspect Sentiment Triplet Extraction (ASTE) aims to identify aspect terms, opinion terms, and sentiment polarities as structured triplets, providing essential inputs for downstream information system applications such as opinion mining, explainable recommendations, and review summarization. Prior work mainly focuses on end-to-end extraction, while post hoc verification of extracted triplets remains comparatively underexplored. This gap limits the reliability of ASTE systems, since predicted triplets may be locally plausible while being globally invalid. Moreover, candidate invalidity is multi-faceted and candidate usability is inherently graded, motivating a fine-grained verification mechanism that can filter or re-rank outputs from diverse extractors. In this paper, we propose FiVeD, a framework for Fine-grained Verification with Diagnostic reasoning supervision. Specifically, the verifier is trained with multiple complementary objectives, including validity classification and quality score estimation as primary tasks, with error type classification and rationale generation as auxiliary tasks. We define hierarchical error categories and construct plausible incorrect triplets under semantic and syntactic constraints, and leverage an off-the-shelf LLM with task-specific rubrics to produce quality scores and diagnostic rationales. During inference, the resulting quality scores are used to filter candidate outputs, supporting adjustable precision-recall tradeoffs. Experiments across multiple ASTE baselines demonstrate that FiVeD consistently improves extraction performance by up to 3.53 F1 points as a plug-and-play verification module.

</details>


### [88] [Translation Analytics for Freelancers II: Benchmarking Local LLMs for Confidential Translation Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31452)
*Yuri Balashov,Rex VanHorn,Mingxi Xu,Austin Downes*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种适用于自由译者和小型语言服务提供商的、低门槛且严谨的翻译技术评估方法，重点解决隐私敏感领域中离线翻译的需求。通过扩展Reeve基金会多语言语料库（RFMC），并在四种语言方向上对多种本地可运行的语言模型进行基准测试，结合自动评估工具MATEO分析性能。结果显示，最佳本地大模型在某些场景下可媲美甚至超越本地NMT系统和前沿大模型GPT-5.2，虽仍逊于顶级商业NMT，但验证了其在隐私约束环境中的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对隐私敏感领域无法使用云端翻译引擎的问题，为自由译者和小型语言服务商提供实用、易行的本地翻译技术评估方案。

Method: 扩展Reeve基金会三语语料库为多语言语料库（RFMC），加入德语和简体中文对照句子；利用Ollama平台在四个语言方向上对多个本地大模型进行单提示无微调的基准测试，每组测试基于1000多个句子；使用MATEO工具进行自动评估，并与商业NMT（如DeepL、百度）、前沿大模型（GPT-5.2）及专业本地NMT系统（OPUS-CAT等）对比性能。

Result: 不同语言方向和模型规模下，本地大模型表现差异显著；部分最优本地大模型性能达到或超过本地NMT系统和GPT-5.2，但整体仍落后于顶级商业NMT（如DeepL）。

Conclusion: 精心选择的本地大模型在隐私受限的专业翻译场景中具备实际可行性，研究结果为模型扩展和多语言能力发展提供了参考依据。

Abstract: Building on our previous work, this paper develops practical, low-barrier methods for freelance translators and smaller language service providers to evaluate translation technologies using rigorous yet accessible analytic methods. Here we address a high-stakes, specialized need: offline translation for confidentiality-sensitive domains in which privacy constraints preclude the use of cloud-based engines and commercial LLMs. We expand the Reeve Foundation Trilingual Corpus (RFTC) used in our previous work into a multilingual corpus (RFMC) by adding sentence-aligned German and Simplified Chinese reference translations. We then benchmark several locally runnable language models (via Ollama) across four language directions on 1000+ sentences selected from this corpus. We use consistent single-prompt calls without fine-tuning or domain adaptation, comparing local LLM outputs against commercial NMTs (DeepL, Baidu), a frontier LLM (GPT-5.2), and professional-grade local NMT systems (OPUS-CAT, NeuralDesktop, Promt). Automatic evaluation is conducted with MATEO. Results reveal substantial variation in local LLM performance across language directions and model sizes. The best local LLMs match or surpass local NMT systems and a frontier LLM, though they remain behind top commercial NMTs. These findings underscore the viability of carefully selected local LLM translation for privacy-constrained professionals and inform future research on model scaling and multilingual capability.

</details>


### [89] [Scaling Conversational Hungarian ASR: The BEA-Dialogue+ Corpus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31469)
*Máté Gedeon,Piroska Zsófia Barta,Péter Mihajlik,Katalin Mády*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了BEA-Dialogue+，一个扩展的匈牙利语对话语音识别语料库，包含200小时转录的自然对话，通过放松数据分割标准解决了训练数据不足的问题，并用于研究额外训练数据与说话人重叠之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于公开可用的匈牙利语对话式训练数据有限，尤其是BEA-Dialogue语料库因严格的说话人互斥划分导致仅能使用85小时数据，限制了对话语音识别的发展，因此需要扩展语料库规模。

Method: 构建BEA-Dialogue+语料库，放宽实验者和对话伙伴的分割限制，同时保持主要说话人完全分离；在此基础上评估基于Whisper和FastConformer的多种模型，包括采用序列化输出训练（SOT）的微调方法。

Result: BEA-Dialogue+达到200小时转录语音；实验表明，更大的语料库对未经微调的模型更具挑战性，但采用SOT微调可显著改善WER、CER、cpWER和cpCER等指标。

Conclusion: BEA-Dialogue+提供了一个更大且仍具挑战性的匈牙利语对话ASR基准，是训练和评估对话转录系统的实用资源。

Abstract: Conversational automatic speech recognition in Hungarian is constrained by the limited amount of publicly available dialogue-style training data. The BEA-Dialogue corpus addresses this need, but its strictly speaker-disjoint train/dev/eval split reduces the usable material to only 85 hours. In this paper, we introduce BEA-Dialogue+, an expanded version of the corpus that relaxes the split criterion for experimenters and dialogue partners while preserving complete separation of the primary speakers. This results in 200 hours of transcribed natural conversations and enables a controlled study of the trade-off between additional training data and speaker overlap across the splits. We evaluate several Whisper- and FastConformer-based models on both corpus versions, including Serialized Output Training (SOT)-based fine-tuning for dialogue transcription. Our results show that the larger corpus is more challenging for models without fine-tuning, whereas SOT-based adaptation yields consistent improvements in WER, CER, cpWER, and cpCER. Overall, BEA-Dialogue+ provides a substantially larger yet still demanding benchmark for Hungarian dialogue ASR, and a practical resource for training and evaluating dialogue transcription systems.

</details>


### [90] [Language Models Can Resolve Reference Compositionally, But It's Not Their Native Strength: The Case of the Personal Relation Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31480)
*Bart Evelo,Meaghan Fowlie,Denis Paperno*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究探讨了大语言模型（LLM）在自然语言组合性理解上的能力，区分了外延任务（指称现实）和内涵任务（结构化表示），发现在人际关系任务中，人类在外延任务上表现更好，而LLM在内涵任务上更优，表明缺乏指称 grounding 是LLM 无法完全模拟人类语言理解的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大语言模型是否真正具备类似人类的组合性语义理解能力，特别是区分指称（外延）与结构表示（内涵）两个维度。

Method: 基于Paperno 2022的个人关系任务，要求模型解析如“Amber's parent's friend”这类短语，分别评估人类和LLM在生成结构公式（内涵任务）和推断实际指称人（外延任务）上的表现。

Result: 人类在外延任务上优于内涵任务，而LLM则相反，在内涵任务上表现更好。

Conclusion: LLM 缺乏现实指称的训练基础，导致其在模拟人类语言理解方面存在根本局限，强调指称 grounding 对实现真正语义理解的重要性。

Abstract: Do neural models, such as Large Language Models, genuinely acquire compositional abilities for interpretation of natural language? When we talk about semantic interpretation, we can distinguish two complementary aspects: establishing what an expression refers to in the world (which we call the Extensional task) and representing its sense in a structured way (which we call the Intensional task). We evaluate LLMs and humans on both tasks in the setting of the Personal Relation Task (Paperno 2022) in which, given a universe of people and their relationships with each other, one is asked to interpret a noun phrase such as "Amber's parent's friend". Here, for the Intensional task, the answer is the formula "friend(parent(amber))", and for the Extensional task, the person. We find that humans and LLMs show opposite strengths: humans perform better on Extensional than Intensional tasks, and LLMs vice versa. Our methodology brings greater nuance to the understanding of compositional abilities in modern machine learning models. Our results support the notion that the lack of referential grounding in LLM training is a crucial missing component in mimicking human-like language understanding.

</details>


### [91] [BenHalluEval: A Multi-Task Hallucination Evaluation Framework for Large Language Models on Bengali](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31483)
*Shefayat E Shams Adib,Ahmed Alfey Sani,Ekramul Alam Esham,Ajwad Abrar,Ishmam Tashdeed,Md Taukir Azam Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了BenHalluEval，首个针对孟加拉语的细粒度幻觉评估框架，涵盖生成式问答、代码混合问答、摘要和推理四项任务。基于GPT-5.4构建了12,000个幻觉样本，评估七种大语言模型，并提出双轨制评估指标BenHalluScore，揭示现有单轨评估与仅依赖提示策略的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管孟加拉语是全球第六大语言，但此前尚无系统性评估大语言模型在该语言中产生幻觉的研究。因此，亟需一个专门针对孟加拉语的幻觉评测基准。

Method: 构建包含12,000个幻觉候选样本的数据集，覆盖三种现有孟加拉语数据集中的十二类任务特定幻觉类型；采用双轨协议评估七类大语言模型：A轨测量真实实例上的误报率，B轨测量幻觉样本上的检出率；提出BenHalluScore作为综合评分指标，并测试思维链提示的缓解效果。

Result: 不同模型和任务下的BenHalluScore介于7.72%至55.42%之间，显示模型在幻觉校准方面表现差异显著；思维链提示改变了响应分布，但未持续提升幻觉辨别能力。

Conclusion: BenHalluEval建立了首个面向孟加拉语的专用幻觉评测基准，揭示了单轨评估和仅依赖提示方法在低资源语言场景下的不足，强调需更精细的多维度评估机制。

Abstract: Despite Bengali being the sixth most spoken language in the world, no prior work has systematically evaluated hallucination in large language models (LLMs) for Bengali. We introduce BenHalluEval, a fine-grained hallucination evaluation framework for Bengali covering four tasks: Generative Question Answering (GQA), Bangla-English Code-Mixed QA, Summarization, and Reasoning. We construct 12,000 hallucinated candidates using GPT-5.4 across twelve task-specific hallucination types, drawn from three existing Bengali datasets, and evaluate seven LLMs spanning reasoning-oriented, multilingual, and Bengali-centric categories under a dual-track protocol that independently measures false-positive rate on ground-truth instances (Track A) and hallucination detection rate on hallucinated candidates (Track B). To jointly penalise both failure modes and prevent inflated scores from uniform response bias, we propose BenHalluScore, a dual-track calibration metric that ranges from 7.72% to 55.42% across models and tasks, revealing substantial variation in hallucination calibration. Chain-of-thought prompting, applied as a mitigation strategy, shifts response distributions without consistently improving hallucination discrimination. BenHalluEval establishes the first dedicated hallucination benchmark for Bengali and highlights the inadequacy of single-track and prompting-only evaluation approaches for low-resource language settings. The dataset and code are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/BanglaHalluEval-EB77.

</details>


### [92] [Are Full Rollouts Necessary for On-Policy Distillation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31490)
*Yaocheng Zhang,Jiajun Chai,Songjun Tu,Yuqian Fu,Xiaohan Wang,Wei Lin,Guojun Yin,Qichao Zhang,Yuanheng Zhu,Dongbin Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出两种控制rollout视野的策略（POPD和TOPD），以提升基于策略蒸馏（OPD）在长视野推理中的训练效率，实验证明新方法在数学推理任务中显著减少计算开销的同时保持性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准OPD在训练时生成完整rollout，计算成本高且可能在早期训练阶段引入不可靠的教师反馈，本文旨在通过控制rollout视野来解决这一瓶颈。

Method: 提出两种视野控制策略：渐进式扩展视野的POPD和始终在截断rollout上进行蒸馏的TOPD。

Result: 在数学推理任务中，POPD将OPD的训练效率提升达3倍，TOPD仅用10%的视野即可达到与OPD相当的性能，大幅降低时间和内存开销。

Conclusion: 控制rollout视野是一种简单且实用的方法，可显著提升OPD的训练效率。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) provides dense teacher feedback along rollouts generated by the student and has emerged as a promising post-training paradigm for long-horizon reasoning. However, standard OPD typically generates full rollouts during training, which is computationally expensive and may expose the student to unreliable teacher feedback at late rollout positions, especially during early training. We identify the rollout horizon as a key bottleneck in OPD that substantially impacts training efficiency. Unlike Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR), OPD does not require a complete trajectory or a final answer reward to provide learning signals. This observation suggests that full rollouts may not always be necessary for effective OPD. Motivated by this insight, we propose two simple horizon-control strategies: Progressive OPD (POPD), which gradually expands the rollout horizon during training, and Truncated OPD (TOPD), which permanently performs distillation on reliable truncated rollouts. Experiments on mathematical reasoning show that POPD improves the training efficiency of OPD by up to 3$\times$, while TOPD matches OPD performance using only 10\% of the rollout horizon, leading to substantial wall-clock and memory reductions. These results demonstrate that controlling the rollout horizon offers a simple and practical path to more efficient OPD.

</details>


### [93] [Consolidating Rewarded Perturbations for LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31494)
*Zheyu Zhang,Shuo Yang,Gjergji Kasneci*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CoRP方法，通过奖励加权聚合和兼容性感知重加权，将RandOpt中多个奖励扰动模型整合为单个可部署模型，在仅一次前向传递下显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的RandOpt方法在推理时需要多次前向传播且难以应用于自由生成任务，本文旨在设计一种无需梯度、高效且可部署的单一模型整合方案。

Method: 基于25个模型-任务对的分析发现低秩结构，提出CoRP算子，结合奖励加权聚合、兼容性感知重加权与验证门控，实现梯度无关的扰动合并。

Result: 在0.5B到8B的五个语言模型和五项任务上，CoRP平均比基础模型提升8.1分；仅用十分之一扰动预算即超越单次推理RandOpt 6.5分，并恢复超过一半的50次集成增益。

Conclusion: CoRP能有效将奖励扰动集成转化为单一高效模型，大幅降低推理成本，同时保持接近多通路集成的性能，适用于实际部署。

Abstract: Post-training of language models is commonly framed as a sample-score-update loop implemented by gradient descent. A recent line of work, exemplified by RandOpt, relocates this loop to weight space, sampling Gaussian perturbations around a pretrained model and ensembling the top-K rewarded specialists at inference. While competitive with PPO and GRPO under matched training compute, this prediction-level ensemble incurs K forward passes per test example and does not extend cleanly to free-form generation. We ask whether the rewarded population can instead be folded into a single deployable model, replacing the inference-time ensemble with one consolidated update. A split-half analysis over 25 model-task pairs reveals reproducible low-rank structure in every case. We turn this geometry into CoRP (Consolidating Rewarded Perturbations), a gradient-free operator that combines reward-weighted aggregation, compatibility-aware reweighting, and a held-out validation gate, with no gradient flowing through the language model. Across five language models from 0.5B to 8B and five tasks covering math, code, and creative writing, CoRP improves the base model by 8.1 points on average. Using one tenth of RandOpt's perturbation budget, CoRP exceeds single-inference RandOpt by 6.5 points and recovers more than half of the gain of the 50-pass majority-vote ensemble, at one forward pass per test example.

</details>


### [94] [Reliable Multilingual Orthopedic Decision Support from Clinical Narratives: Language-Aware Adaptation and Verification-Guided Deferral](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31512)
*Danish Ali,Li Xiaojian,Sundas Iqbal,Farrukh Zaidi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向低资源医疗环境的多语言骨科决策支持框架，通过改进的IndicBERT-HPA模型实现跨语言临床文本分类，并引入选择性验证机制提升系统可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在低资源医疗环境中，骨科临床文本常包含专业术语、混合脚本、证据不全、标签不平衡和语言依赖模式，现有方法难以实现可靠且公平的多语言决策支持。

Method: 提出IndicBERT-HPA模型，基于IndicBERT并添加语言感知的骨科适配器头；比较多种多语言编码器与零样本大语言模型；设计结合置信度门控、证据一致性检查和语言风险筛查的确定性选择性验证层。

Result: 在自然临床分布下，IndicBERT-HPA取得最优性能：平均Macro-F1为0.8792，Macro-AUROC为0.894，AUPRC为0.902；选择性验证层在72.3%覆盖下达到84.4%准确率和0.76 Macro-F1，优于全接受预测。

Conclusion: 任务适配的编码器优于零样本大模型，结合显式拒绝机制的选择性验证可有效提升多语言临床决策系统的可靠性与稳健性。

Abstract: Multilingual orthopedic decision support remains challenging in low-resource healthcare settings, where clinical narratives contain specialized terminology, mixed scripts, incomplete evidence, label imbalance and language-dependent documentation patterns. This article presents a reliability-oriented framework for classifying free-text orthopedic notes in English, Hindi and Punjabi. We compare task-aligned multilingual transformer encoders, a task-fine-tuned DistilBERT baseline, zero-shot instruction-tuned large language models (LLMs) and a domain-adaptive encoder, IndicBERT-HPA. IndicBERT-HPA augments IndicBERT with language-aware orthopedic adapter heads to support clinically relevant multilingual representation learning. Evaluation extends beyond aggregate accuracy to per-class performance, ROC-AUC, AUPRC, expected calibration error, cross-language stability and robustness under controlled balanced and natural-prevalence distributions. The evaluated zero-shot LLMs remain substantially less effective than task-adapted encoders for closed-set classification, with language-dependent instability. Under natural clinical prevalence, IndicBERT-HPA achieves the strongest overall performance, reaching an averaged Macro-F1 of 0.8792, Macro-AUROC of 0.894 and AUPRC of 0.902. We further implement a deterministic selective-verification layer combining confidence gating, evidence-consistency checking and language-risk screening. On a randomly selected held-out 5,000-record subset, it achieves 84.4% selective accuracy and 0.76 selective Macro-F1 at 72.3% coverage, compared with 71.5% accuracy and 0.65 Macro-F1 for accept-all prediction. These results support reliability-oriented multilingual clinical decision support with explicit deferral.

</details>


### [95] [UniAudio-Token: Empowering Semantic Speech Tokenizers with General Audio Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31521)
*Yuhan Song,Linhao Zhang,Aiwei Liu,Chuhan Wu,Sijun Zhang,Wei Jia,Yuan Liu,Houfeng Wang,Xiao Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: UniAudio-Token 是一种新型音频分词框架，能够在保持语义分词器语言能力的同时增强其对非语音音频的感知能力，通过引入语义-声学基元和内容感知门控机制实现通用音频表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的语义语音分词器专注于语言抽象，导致对声学特征不敏感，限制了其在非语音任务中的应用。因此需要一种既能保留语音语言理解能力又能捕捉丰富声学信息的统一音频表示方法。

Method: 提出 UniAudio-Token 框架，包含两个关键创新：一是语义-声学基元（SAP），将音频分解为语言内容、发声属性和听觉场景基元以提供结构化监督；二是语义-声学均衡（SAE），通过内容感知的门控机制从浅层自适应恢复细粒度声学细节。

Result: 实验表明，UniAudio-Token 在理解和生成任务上均优于所有单码本基线分词器，能够学习到全面的通用表示并保持高保真语音生成能力。

Conclusion: UniAudio-Token 成功扩展了语义分词器的能力边界，使其成为适用于多种音频任务的统一接口，具有良好的实际应用前景。

Abstract: Semantic speech tokenizers have become a widely used interface for Audio-LLMs, owing to their compact single-codebook design and strong linguistic alignment. However, their focus on linguistic abstraction induces acoustic blindness, limiting their applicability beyond speech-centric tasks. We propose UniAudio-Token, a framework that empowers semantic tokenizers with general audio perception without compromising speech ability. Instead of altering the semantic paradigm, UniAudio-Token mitigates its information loss through two key innovations: (1) Semantic-Acoustic Primitives (SAP) provide structured supervision by decomposing audio into linguistic content, vocal attributes, and auditory-scene primitives; and (2) Semantic-Acoustic Equilibrium (SAE) introduces a content-aware gating mechanism that adaptively restores fine-grained acoustic details from shallow layers. Extensive evaluations show that UniAudio-Token learns comprehensive universal representations while preserving high-fidelity speech generation. When integrated with downstream LLMs, it outperforms all single-codebook baseline tokenizers on both understanding and generation tasks, effectively serving as a unified audio interface. We publicly release all our code, including training and inference scripts, together with the model checkpoints at https://github.com/Tencent/Universal_Audio_Tokenizer.

</details>


### [96] [Semantic Triplet Restoration: A Novel Protocol for Hierarchical Table Understanding in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31550)
*Yibin Zhao,Fangxin Shang,Dingrui Yang,Yuqi Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为语义三元组恢复（STR）的新协议，用于表格问答任务，通过将每个单元格重写为原子事实三元组来显式建模二维布局、合并单元格和层次化表头中的语义关系，并结合轻量级查询感知路由器TripletQL，在减少输入token的同时，在多个中英文基准上达到或超过了基于HTML的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格问答方法使用HTML或Markdown等布局导向的序列化格式作为中间表示，存在标记开销大、需要模型自行推断表头与单元格对齐关系的问题，尤其在小模型和长表格上下文中表现受限，因此需要一种更高效、语义更明确的表示方法。

Method: 提出语义三元组恢复（STR）协议，将每个单元格转换为<item path, feature path, value>形式的原子事实三元组，其中item path表示行实体，feature path表示层次属性，value为单元格内容；并设计TripletQL路由器，根据问题选择合适的三元组渲染或子集输入给语言模型。

Result: 在四个中英文表格问答基准上，STR在匹配或优于基于HTML基线的同时减少了输入token数量，且在小语言模型和长表格上下文中相对优势更明显。

Conclusion: 显式的语义表示（如STR）能有效提升表格问答性能，尤其在推理资源受限的情况下更具优势，表明剥离布局复杂性、直接建模语义关系是一种更高效的路径。

Abstract: Table question answering requires models to recover semantic relations encoded implicitly by two-dimensional layout, merged cells, and hierarchical headers. Current pipelines typically use HTML or Markdown as intermediate table representations, but these layout-oriented serializations introduce markup overhead and require large language models to infer header-cell alignments from row and column spans. We propose Semantic Triplet Restoration (STR), a protocol that rewrites each cell as an atomic fact <item path, feature path, value>, where the item path specifies the row-wise entity, the feature path specifies the hierarchical attribute, and the value contains the cell content. We also present TripletQL, a lightweight query-aware router that uses STR to select an appropriate rendering or filtered subset of triplets for each question. Across four Chinese and English table-QA benchmarks, STR matches or improves upon HTML-based baselines while reducing input tokens. The relative benefit grows for smaller language models and longer table contexts, suggesting that explicit semantic representations are especially useful under constrained inference budgets. Code and data are available at https://github.com/Phoenix-ni/STR.git .

</details>


### [97] [What Gets Unmasked First? Trajectory Analysis of Diffusion Models for Graph-to-Text Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31564)
*Qing Wang,Jacob Devasier,Chengkai Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了掩码扩散语言模型（MDLMs）在图到文本生成中的应用，发现其解码过程中优先生成实体词，随后是关系词和功能词，结构词最后生成。研究揭示了监督微调（SFT）会过早固定句尾结构词，导致生成长度受限、信息遗漏或幻觉的问题。为此提出了一种无需训练的推理策略——lambda-scaled structural decoding，有效提升BLEU-4达9.4。同时引入Graph-LLaDA模型，通过图Transformer编码器增强结构信息建模，在跨数据集实验中展现出更强泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管MDLM在图像和语音生成中表现优异，但其在图到文本生成中的应用尚未被系统研究；同时，传统监督微调可能破坏MDLM自然的生成策略，影响生成质量，因此需要深入分析并改进。

Method: 分析MDLM的生成轨迹，识别SFT带来的问题；提出lambda-scaled structural decoding方法，在推理时降低结构词的置信度权重；设计Graph-LLaDA模型，将Graph Transformer编码器融入LLaDA解码过程以显式建模图结构信息。

Result: 发现MDLM优先生成实体而非线性生成；SFT会导致结构词过早固化，限制输出灵活性；所提lambda-scaled方法在不需再训练的情况下显著提升BLEU-4分数（+9.4）；Graph-LLaDA结合图结构信息后，在LAGRANGE跨数据集上表现出更优的泛化性能。

Conclusion: MDLM具有符合语义层级的自然生成顺序；SFT可能损害该优势，而简单的推理调整即可恢复性能；显式建模图结构有助于提升模型泛化能力，为未来非自回归图到文本生成提供了新方向。

Abstract: We present the first systematic study of masked diffusion language models (MDLMs) for graph-to-text generation. We analyze MDLM generation trajectories -- the order in which tokens are unmasked during iterative decoding -- and find that, unlike autoregressive LLMs which generate text linearly, MDLMs naturally prioritize entities first, followed by relational and function words, with structural tokens resolved last. We further identify a previously undocumented failure mode of supervised fine-tuning: SFT disrupts this strategy by prematurely anchoring structural sentence-ending tokens early in the decoding trajectory, effectively fixing the output length which can lead to omitted or hallucinated information. To address this, we propose lambda-scaled structural decoding, a training-free inference-time modification that downweights structural token confidence and recovers +9.4 BLEU-4. Finally, we introduce Graph-LLaDA, which integrates a Graph Transformer encoder into LLaDA's decoding process to explicitly incorporate relational graph structure. Cross-dataset evaluation on LAGRANGE reveals that previous baselines overfit to dataset-specific patterns, while LLM- and MDLM-based approaches generalize significantly better.

</details>


### [98] [What Am I Missing? Question-Answering as Hidden State Probing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31561)
*Chu Fei Luo,Samuel Dahan,Xiaodan Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨了大型语言模型在测试时推理过程中通过提问进行干预的机制，提出学生-教师框架下的问题生成可作为对模型隐藏状态的自我诊断，并发现尽管能有效检测正确性与不确定性，但干预措施在修正错误方面效果有限，揭示了诊断与纠正之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究大型语言模型在相同输入和部分解下仍产生不同输出的现象，探索其推理过程中的内在机制，特别是如何利用问题生成来揭示模型隐藏状态的信息。

Method: 采用学生-教师设置，训练探针分析学生模型在提问前后隐藏状态的变化，将其用于预测推理轨迹的最终正确性，并将提问建模为序贯决策问题，设计基于探针评分的门控策略以优化提问时机。

Result: 探针能在教师回答生成前预测推理路径的正确性，表明提问过程本身包含自我诊断信号；然而实验显示干预既可能破坏正确路径也可能修复错误路径，诊断与恢复之间存在明显差距。

Conclusion: 问题生成作为推理时干预的有效性高度依赖于模型的自洽性，当前方法虽能捕捉不确定性，但在主动修正错误方面仍有局限，反映出语言模型在不确定下自我精炼能力的挑战。

Abstract: Test-time reasoning has become a significant field of study since the introduction of chain-of-thought reasoning in large language models (LLMs). However, the mechanisms of this reasoning process are still under-explored -- from the same input prompt, and even the same partial solution, LLMs can produce varied answers if sampled multiple times. We propose to leverage question-asking as an inference-time intervention that articulates information about the model's hidden state. To achieve that, we present a student-teacher setting where a student asks questions to a teacher. We train a probe on the student's hidden state before and after asking a question and find it is predictive of the trajectory's final correctness, even before generating the teacher's answer. This suggests there is a meaningful signal from the self-diagnosis that occurs during question generation rather than information transfer from the teacher. We then frame question-asking as a sequential decision problem, using this probe as a quality score, and define a gating policy to ask questions that maximize likelihood of correctness. We find that the success of question-asking as an intervention is largely dependent on the model's self-consistency. Our empirical results show a gap between detection and recovery; while our gating policy captures model correctness and uncertainty, interventions are equally likely to harm correct trajectories as they are to recover incorrect ones. This gap between diagnosis and correction has broader implications on language models' capacity for self-refinement under uncertainty.

</details>


### [99] [Disagreeing Rationales: Rethinking Classification and Explainability Evaluation in Hate Speech Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31563)
*Benedetta Muscato,Beiduo Chen,Gizem Gezici,Barbara Plank,Fosca Giannotti*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过统一不同模型、训练策略和评估指标，系统地重新实现了标签和解释的多种表示方法，揭示了软性表示在捕捉主观NLP任务中人类判断变异性方面的优势，并强调需重新思考分类与可解释性评估方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类在标注过程中存在广泛分歧，尤其是在主观性强的自然语言处理任务（如仇恨言论检测）中，现有的标签和解释评估与聚合方法不足以反映这种多样性，因此需要更全面的评估框架来理解人类推理的丰富性。

Method: 研究整合了多种模型、训练策略、损失函数和评估指标，在统一框架下对硬性和软性标签及解释表示空间进行系统性重实现，并从预测性和分布性两个维度评估分类性能，从可信性、忠实性和复杂性三个维度评估可解释性。

Result: 实验结果表明，无论是硬性还是软性指标，均更偏好软性表示方式，说明其能更有效地捕捉人类标注中的变异性和细微差异。

Conclusion: 当前主流的多数投票和硬性标签方法可能掩盖了人类判断的多样性，应采用更灵活的软性表示和评估方法以更好地反映主观任务中的真实认知差异。

Abstract: Human disagreement is ubiquitous and well-known in labeling. However, variation in explanations, captured through token-level human rationales, remains far less explored. At the same time, it is unclear how to best evaluate human labels and rationales -- or even how to best aggregate rationales beyond majority vote -- in light of this variation. Yet, rationales may provide additional insights into the richness of human reasoning, that may differ in style, values and interpretations -- especially in subjective NLP tasks like hate speech detection. In this work, we unify diverse models, training strategies, loss functions, and existing evaluation metrics under a single protocol by systematically re-implementing them across different label and rationale representation spaces. Classification metrics are organized around two key properties -- predictive and distributional -- while explainability metrics through three complementary dimensions: plausibility, faithfulness, and complexity. In this unified supervision framework, we evaluate model behavior across classification and explainability metrics, as well as metric sensitivity to the choice of label (hard and soft) and rationale representation space (hard, intermediate and soft). Results show that both hard and soft metrics favor softer representations, highlighting their effectiveness in capturing variation and the need to rethink evaluation in subjective NLP.

</details>


### [100] [Language Models Learn Constructional Semantics, Not To Mention Syntax: Investigating LM Understanding of Paired-Focus Constructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31586)
*Wesley Scivetti,Ethan Wilcox,Nathan Schneider,Kanishka Misra,Leonie Weissweiler*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究构建了一个新数据集来测试英语中罕见的“配对焦点”结构（如“let alone”，“much less”）的语义理解，发现一些中等规模的开源语言模型能够掌握这些结构的形式与意义，但依赖人类规模数据训练的模型在语义任务上表现不佳；语义理解在训练后期出现，并与世界知识的学习相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究开源语言模型是否具备对罕见构式（form-meaning pairing）的稳健理解能力，以及这种知识的习得背后的学习动态。

Method: 构建针对英语中罕见Paired-Focus结构的新数据集，结合标量形容词语义和常识知识设计评测任务，评估不同参数量、架构和训练数据规模的模型表现，并分析开放检查点模型的训练动态。

Result: 多个中等规模模型能敏感地识别Paired-Focus结构的形式与意义，但使用人类规模数据训练的模型在所有语义评估中均失败；语义理解在训练过程中晚于句法知识出现，且其学习与某些领域世界知识的增长呈正相关。

Conclusion: 中等规模的开源模型可以掌握罕见的Paired-Focus构式，且构式语义理解与世界知识等语义领域存在关联。

Abstract: Grasping the semantics of rare constructions (form-meaning pairings) has been shown to be a challenging problem that has currently only been solved by the largest LLMs. It remains an open question if open-source models have robust constructional understanding, and if so, what learning dynamics underlie the acquisition of this knowledge. Focusing on a set of rare Paired-Focus constructions in English (e.g. "let alone", "much less"), we construct a novel dataset to test their meanings using both scalar adjectival semantics and general world knowledge. Testing a wide range of models differing in parameter count, architecture, and pretraining dataset size, we find that several modestly sized models are sensitive to both the forms and the meanings of Paired-Focus constructions, though models trained on human-scale data fail at all meaning evaluations. Turning to training dynamics for a set of open-checkpoint models, we find that Paired-Focus understanding emerges later in training than Paired-Focus syntactic knowledge, and that learning of Paired-Focus semantics is correlated with gains in some domains of world knowledge. Overall, our empirical results support the conclusion that modestly sized open-source models can grasp the rare Paired-Focus constructions, and demonstrate a connection between knowledge of Paired-Focus constructions and other meaning domains.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [101] [PhyDrawGen: Physically Grounded Diagram Generation from Natural Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30512)
*Nafiul Haque,Syed Nazmus Sakib,Shifat E Arman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PhyDrawGen 是一个神经符号系统，通过解耦语义理解与物理约束满足，生成符合物理定律的图表，显著优于现有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型在生成物理图表时常常违反物理定律，如力的守恒、几何约束等，缺乏物理准确性。

Method: 首先使用大语言模型从文本中提取类型化的场景图，然后通过确定性求解器将其转换为平面直线图（PSLG），最后利用微调的Qwen-VL模型进行视觉引导的提出-验证循环以修正约束违规。

Result: 在包含1449个问题的基准测试中，PhyDrawGen 在力学、光学和电磁学领域均显著优于 GPT-5-image、Gemini 2.5 Flash 和 Gemini 3 Pro。

Conclusion: PhyDrawGen 能够有效保证生成图表的物理正确性，尤其在处理非常规物体问题时表现出强健的性能。

Abstract: Generating physics diagrams from text requires strict adherence to physical laws. While current generative models produce visually plausible outputs, they systematically hallucinate force vectors, ignore conservation laws, and violate geometric constraints. We present PhyDrawGen, a neuro-symbolic pipeline that decouples semantic scene understanding from physical constraint satisfaction. First, a large language model extracts a typed scene graph from the problem text. A deterministic solver then converts this graph into a Planar Straight-Line Graph (PSLG), encoding force balance, optical paths, and field topologies as exact geometric primitives. Finally, a fine-tuned Qwen-VL model implements a visually grounded propose-verify loop to iteratively correct any constraint violations. Evaluated on a benchmark of 1,449 problems spanning mechanics, optics, and electromagnetism, PhyDrawGen significantly outperforms GPT-5-image, Gemini 2.5 Flash, and Gemini 3 Pro, demonstrating robust physical accuracy even on unusual-object problems.

</details>


### [102] [Physically Viable World Models: A Case for Query-Conditioned Embodied AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30542)
*Adam J. Thorpe,Stepan Tretiakov,Cheng-Hsi Hsiao,Su Ann Low,Xingjian Li,Hassan Iqbal,Neel P. Bhatt,Ufuk Topcu,Krishna Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出，具身人工智能的世界模型必须具备物理可行性，即能够通过表示决定干预结果的物理结构来回答干预性问题，而不仅仅是预测未来的观测。现有基于观测预测的模型可能产生视觉上合理但物理上错误的推演。作者通过控制实验揭示了这一结构性缺陷，并提出一种模块化、可解释且可验证的世界模型框架，强调应根据查询动态构建最简化的物理抽象模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的世界模型仅依赖观测预测，无法正确处理干预性问题，导致在具身AI中推荐不可行或不安全的行为。因此需要构建能反映真实物理结构、支持干预推理的新型世界模型。

Method: 提出一种模块化世界模型架构，包含环境表示、状态与参数估计、动作定义、干预动力学和查询响应等组件；引入自主协调器动态组合学习与结构化模块，以构建针对特定查询的最简物理抽象模型。

Result: 在控制变量的基准测试中，现有观察预测模型出现物理错误的推演和不安全决策；新框架能准确回答干预性查询，避免物理不合理行为，并支持可解释性和可验证性。

Conclusion: 具身AI需要基于物理结构的、可干预推理的世界模型，而非仅依赖外观预测的模型。正确的抽象不是最详细的，而是保留查询相关物理区分的最简模型，该原则为世界模型的设计与评估提供了新标准。

Abstract: World models for embodied AI must be physically viable: constructed to answer intervention queries by representing the physical structure governing action outcomes, rather than merely predicting future observations. Existing observation-predictive world models can produce visually plausible but physically wrong rollouts. This failure is structural; distinct physical systems can look identical yet diverge under intervention. We expose this problem with controlled benchmarks that fix the visible scene while varying latent physics. We show that such models may recommend infeasible actions, mispredict interaction outcomes, or certify unsafe behavior. We argue that embodied AI requires world models that identify the simplest physical abstraction sufficient to answer an intervention query. Such a model comprises modular components, including environment representation, latent state and parameter estimation, action specification, interventional dynamics, and query-level response. An autonomous orchestrator should identify the relevant abstraction and compose compatible learned and structured components per query. When closed-form physics is unavailable, uncertain, or costly, the transition model may be analytic, simulated, learned, or hybrid, but it must preserve the structure that determines interventional outcomes. This decomposition makes the model interpretable, its components verifiable, and its outputs auditable against the query. It also provides a design principle for new world models and a feasibility test for existing ones: the right abstraction is not the most detailed model of the world, but the simplest model that preserves the distinctions relevant to the query. We demonstrate this approach on queries that existing systems fail to answer correctly, and outline how an orchestrator can dynamically assemble and adapt physically viable models for planning, control, and verification.

</details>


### [103] [Transforming and Encoding FTS for SAT Solving: What Helps, What Hurts (Extended Version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30563)
*João Filipe,Álvaro Torralba,Gregor Behnke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了如何将具有复杂条件和非确定性的分解任务编码为SAT问题，提出了多种转换策略，并分析了并行性和任务变换对基于SAT的规划器性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了更紧凑地表示规划任务并支持多种任务转换，现有的分解任务表示法虽优于STRIPS或SAS+，但目前仅限于启发式搜索方法，缺乏对SAT求解器的有效支持。

Method: 提出多种将分解转移关系转化为命题逻辑的编码方法，探讨在不同层次上利用并行性的策略，并评估常见任务变换对SAT规划器性能的影响。

Result: 实现了将分解任务有效编码为SAT问题的方法，展示了不同编码策略的效果，并揭示了并行化和任务变换对求解效率的影响。

Conclusion: 基于SAT的方法可以有效处理分解任务，合理的编码策略与并行优化能够显著提升规划性能，拓展了传统规划形式的能力。

Abstract: Factored tasks are a classical planning representation that extends SAS+ with limited forms of disjunctive preconditions, conditional effects, and angelic nondeterminism. This allows for a more compact representation of tasks than traditional formalisms such as STRIPS or SAS+, and supports a wide range of task transformations. However, existing planning approaches for factored tasks have been limited to heuristic search methods.
  In this work, we investigate how to encode factored tasks in SAT. We propose several ways to encode the tasks, focusing on different strategies for translating the factored transition relation into propositional logic. We also analyze how to exploit parallelism at various levels in this setting and study the impact of common task transformations on the performance of SAT-based planners.

</details>


### [104] [Procedural Generation of First Person Shooter Maps using Map-Elites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30570)
*Simone de Donato,Pier Luca Lanzi,Daniele Loiacono*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了使用MAP-Elites算法结合新提出的地图表示方法（Point-Line和Spatial-Layout）来生成多样性和质量更高的第一人称射击游戏关卡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提升FPS游戏关卡设计的多样性和质量，探索适用于质量多样性优化算法MAP-Elites的地图表示方法。

Method: 引入两种新的地图表示方法（Point-Line和Spatial-Layout），并与传统的All-Black和Grid-Graph表示进行比较；定义描述地图拓扑和涌现特性的指标，并利用MAP-Elites with Sliding Boundaries（MESB）算法演化FPS地图种群。

Result: 新的地图表示方法能够生成比以往表示更高多样性和质量的FPS游戏地图。

Conclusion: Point-Line和Spatial-Layout表示法在指导MAP-Elites算法生成优质多样的FPS关卡方面更为有效，为游戏内容生成提供了新的方向。

Abstract: We investigate the application of MAP-Elites (a well-known quality diversity algorithm) to design levels for First-Person Shooter (FPS) games. We consider two well-known map representations (All-Black and Grid-Graph) and introduce two novel representations (Point-Line and Spatial-Layout) that improve the characterization of FPS maps. We define a series of metrics to describe maps' topological properties (which solely depend on maps' layout), and emergent properties (which must be evaluated through actual gameplay). We perform an in-depth analysis to identify the most suitable features to guide MAP-Elites illumination process. We apply MAP-Elites with Sliding Boundaries (MESB) to evolve populations of FPS maps. Our results show that the new representations can generate maps with higher diversity and quality than the representations previously used for evolving FPS maps.

</details>


### [105] [EHRBench: An Automated and Reliable EHR-based Benchmark for Clinical Decision Making with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30637)
*Yuzhang Xie,Keqi Han,Yunpeng Xiao,Hejie Cui,Guanchen Wu,Ziyang Zhang,Kai Shu,Jiaying Lu,Xiao Hu,Carl Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了EHRBench，一个基于电子健康记录（EHR）的大规模、自动化且可靠的基准，用于评估基于大语言模型（LLM）的临床决策能力，涵盖诊断、治疗和预后三大任务，包含近百万个问答项，并通过LLM与知识库协同验证提升可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的临床决策评估基准在规模和质量上存在不足，难以有效评估大语言模型在真实临床决策任务中的可靠性；缺乏基于真实患者EHR、需要医学推理的高质量、大规模评测集。

Method: 提出EHR-LLM-KB交互管道：利用专用LLM将EHR轨迹自动转化为结构化模板并生成问答项，同时通过知识库进行系统性验证与增强，过滤幻觉和模糊关系，确保数据质量与可靠性。

Result: 构建了包含960,067个问答项的EHRBench基准，覆盖诊断、治疗和预后任务；对超过30个主流LLM进行了评测，发现模型性能具有一致趋势，验证了基准的可靠性，并揭示了当前LLM在临床应用中的可改进差距。

Conclusion: EHRBench是一个可扩展、高质量的临床决策评估基准，能够有效评估LLM在真实医学推理任务中的表现，为开发更可靠的临床AI系统提供了重要工具和方向。

Abstract: Clinical decision-making (CDM) is central to real-world clinical workflows, where clinicians infer diagnoses, select treatments, or anticipate future health outcomes under incomplete evidence. LLMs are increasingly used to support these decisions due to strong language capabilities, broad biomedical knowledge, and efficiency, yet the reliability of LLMs on real-world clinical decision tasks remains insufficiently understood. To evaluate CDM models, especially LLM-based models, an ideal and practical medical decision benchmark should be constructed via an automated yet reliable pipeline to ensure both scale and quality. Moreover, the grounding of a CDM benchmark in real patient EHRs can better support evaluation on practical CDM tasks that require substantive biomedical knowledge and clinical inference. To fill the gaps, we introduce EHRBench, an automated and reliable EHR-grounded benchmark for evaluating LLM-based clinical decision-making at scale. To ensure scalability and reliability, EHRBench is constructed through an EHR-LLM-KB(knowledge-base) interaction pipeline. For efficiency, we use a specialized LLM to automatically convert encounter-level EHR trajectories into structured templates and deterministically instantiate the templates into QA items. In parallel, we apply systematic KB-based verification and enrichment to filter hallucinated or ambiguous relations and to improve reliability. Using this pipeline, we construct nearly 1M (960,067) QA items spanning three core inference-required clinical decision tasks: diagnosis, treatment, and prognosis. We benchmark more than 30 representative LLMs on EHRBench and provide detailed analyses of performance and robustness. The results show consistent capability trends across settings, further validating the reliability of EHRBench and highlighting actionable gaps toward clinically reliable LLM systems.

</details>


### [106] [Structure-Induced Information for Rerooting Levin Tree Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30664)
*Jake Tuero,Michael Buro,Laurent Orseau,Levi H. S. Lelis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于学习的“重根器”（rerooter）方法，通过隐式分解问题为软子任务，克服了传统子目标搜索中显式生成子目标带来的计算开销和可扩展性差的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于子目标的策略树搜索依赖显式子目标生成，导致计算开销大、难以扩展。本文旨在通过隐式任务分解提升搜索效率与可扩展性。

Method: 采用√LTS算法框架，提出三种重根器设计：基于聚类的、基于启发式（利用学到的代价估计）、以及两者的混合形式，实现无需显式构建子目标的搜索引导。

Result: 所提方法在复杂环境中表现出良好可扩展性，在线训练效率达到当前最优水平，且在传统方法失效的场景中仍能有效运行。

Conclusion: 基于学习的重根器能有效隐式分解任务，显著降低计算开销，提升了策略树搜索在复杂确定性问题中的可扩展性和效率。

Abstract: Subgoal-based policy tree search, which uses a policy to guide search, is effective for complex single-agent deterministic problems but often relies on explicit subgoal generation that can incur substantial overhead and hinders scalability. In this paper, we overcome these limitations by using a learned ``rerooter'' through the recently-introduced $\sqrt{\text{LTS}}$ algorithm. A rerooter implicitly decomposes the problem into soft subtasks. While previous work focused on the formal guarantees for given or handcrafted rerooters, in this work we propose three rerooter designs: (i) a clustering-based rerooter that exploits global state-space structure, (ii) a heuristic-based rerooter that leverages learned cost-to-go estimates, and (iii) a hybrid that combines both signals. Our framework avoids having to explicitly reconstruct and reason over generated subgoals, thereby enabling scalable allocation of search effort with significantly lower computational overhead. Empirically, our rerooting-based methods scale to complex environments where subgoal-based policy tree search fails, and achieve state-of-the-art online training efficiency on the domains tested.

</details>


### [107] [Healthcare Mechanisms from Policy-as-Code Search under Strategic Provider Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30680)
*Zihan Wang,Xiang Xu,Hongyuan Zha,Wenhao Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于程序合成和多智能体模拟的医院机制设计新方法，通过可解释的规则程序与战略性提供者行为模拟，评估医疗AI机制在均衡状态下的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗AI基准未能考虑医疗服务提供者的策略性反应，因此无法真实反映机制设计在实际运行中产生的均衡效果，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 将医院机制设计转化为面向语言模型的程序合成问题，使用具有五种策略通道（编码、选择、延迟、努力、分诊）的多智能体模拟器Medi-Sim执行并评分可检查的类型化规则程序，并利用LLM引导的进化代码搜索优化规则。

Result: 激励扫描重现了经典卫生经济学现象，如盈利压力下的过度编码和偏好低复杂度患者；单一审计手段揭示了压力迁移现象；最终合成的混合目标规则程序有效消除过度编码，使拒绝率减半，并保留大部分资金效率。

Conclusion: 该框架支持可解释、可评估且能应对策略性行为的医疗机制设计，为未来医疗政策与AI系统的协同优化提供了新路径。

Abstract: Healthcare mechanisms are inseparable from the strategic provider response they induce: existing healthcare AI benchmarks hold this response fixed and so cannot evaluate mechanisms by the equilibrium they produce. We recast hospital mechanism design as program synthesis for language models: typed, inspectable rule programs are executed and scored by Medi-Sim, a multi-agent simulator with five strategic provider channels (coding, selection, delay, effort, triage). An incentive sweep recovers classical health-economics findings as adjacent regimes -- up-coding and low-complexity-patient selection under profit pressure, and Goodhart-style drift where measured performance becomes anti-correlated with true outcomes -- and a single audit lever exposes pressure migration: closing the coding channel more than doubles low-complexity selection. LLM-guided evolutionary code search over the same rule-program space then synthesizes an inspectable mixed-objective program that eliminates up-coding, halves rejection, and retains most of the profit-oriented baseline's funds.

</details>


### [108] [Generating Graph-like Rules for Knowledge Graph Reasoning via Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30747)
*Haoxiang Cheng,Yunfei Wang,Chao Chen,Kewei Cheng,Zhipeng Lin,Haoxuan Li,Changjun Fan,Shixuan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为GRiD的框架，用于从知识图谱中发现图结构规则，通过结合监督预训练和强化学习来克服传统方法在搜索空间和不可微质量指标上的局限性，并在多个基准数据集上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有规则挖掘方法主要关注链式规则，忽略了知识图谱中更丰富的图结构信息（如环和分支），且面临组合爆炸带来的计算瓶颈；同时，生成模型因训练目标与规则质量指标不一致而难以直接应用。

Method: 将图状规则发现建模为基于目标关系的条件离散生成过程，采用两阶段训练策略：首先进行监督预训练以学习子图结构先验，然后使用强化学习通过策略梯度优化，直接利用不可微的规则质量指标进行指导。

Result: 在六个基准数据集上的实验表明，GRiD在知识图谱补全任务中具有竞争力的表现，消融研究验证了其高效性和鲁棒性，并显示图状规则能够有效补充链状规则。

Conclusion: GRiD成功实现了高质量图状逻辑规则的生成，突破了传统方法对简单结构的依赖以及生成模型在规则挖掘中的应用障碍，为知识图谱推理提供了更具表达力和解释性的工具。

Abstract: Logical rules constitute a cornerstone of knowledge graph (KG) reasoning, valued for their interpretability and ability to model relational patterns. However, existing rule mining methods predominantly focus on simple chain-like rules and therefore neglect the richer relational information encoded in graph-like structures, such as cycles and branches. This limitation is further exacerbated by computational bottlenecks caused by the combinatorial explosion of the search space, which is especially challenging for graph-like rules. Meanwhile, generative approaches such as diffusion models, despite their success in other domains, can not be directly applied to rule mining because their training objectives are not aligned with the goal of learning high-quality rules, and non-differentiable KG rule quality metrics cannot directly guide model optimization. To address these limitations, we propose GRiD, a framework that reformulates graph-like rule discovery as a discrete generative process conditioned on the target relation. GRiD employs a two-phase training strategy. First, supervised pre-training enables GRiD to capture structural priors from subgraphs sampled from the KG meta-graph. Subsequently, reinforcement learning is applied to fine-tune GRiD through policy gradient optimization guided directly by non-differentiable rule-quality metrics. Experiments on six benchmark datasets show that GRiD achieves competitive performance on KG completion tasks. Ablation studies confirm the efficiency and robustness of GRiD and further show that graph-like rules complement chain-like rules in KG completion. Our codes and datasets are available in https://github.com/Haoxiang-Cheng/GRiD

</details>


### [109] [Planner-Centric Reinforcement Learning for Deep Research with Structure-Aware Reward](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30824)
*Mustafa Anis Hussain,Xinle Wu,Yao Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为DecomposeR的深度研究框架，通过将研究计划表示为有向无环图（DAG），实现对规划过程的显式建模与优化，并采用两阶段强化学习提升大模型在长文本问答中的规划与回答能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的训练范式依赖短答案任务或整体长轨迹优化，难以分离规划与执行过程，导致规划环节的信用分配不足，影响深度研究任务的表现。

Method: 提出DecomposeR框架，将研究计划建模为类型化有向无环图（DAG）；采用两阶段强化学习：先对规划器进行图结构与查询分解的训练，再训练回答器基于生成的计划进行分支执行与最终合成；通过为显式的规划标记和结构化组件分配奖励，实现更细粒度的优化。

Result: 实验表明，基于Qwen3-8B的DecomposeR在多个流行的长文本问答基准上比强开源基线高出5.1-8.0分，验证了其在规划与回答能力上的提升。

Conclusion: DecomposeR通过结构化、可奖励的规划机制，有效提升了大语言模型在复杂深度研究任务中的表现，为长文本推理任务提供了新的训练范式。

Abstract: Deep research tasks require LLMs to plan what to investigate, retrieve evidence, and synthesize long-form answers across multiple branches of inquiry. Existing training paradigms either rely on short-form verifiable QA as a proxy or optimize monolithic long trajectories, which makes planning and execution difficult to disentangle and yields weak credit assignment for the planning process. We propose DecomposeR, a planner-centric deep research framework that represents research plans as typed directed acyclic graphs (DAGs), allowing planning to be made explicit, structured, and rewardable. We train a Qwen3-8B model in two stages: planner reinforcement learning (RL) first learns graph structure and query decomposition to improve research planning, and answerer reinforcement learning (RL) then learns branch-level execution and final synthesis conditioned on the learned plan. By assigning rewards to explicit planner tokens and structured components rather than to a flat trajectory, DecomposeR enables finer-grained optimization of planning while reducing the ambiguity of end-to-end training. Experiments show that DecomposeR-8B improves over strong comparable open baselines by 5.1-8.0 points on popular long-form benchmarks due to improved planning and answering capabilities.

</details>


### [110] [SLAT: Segment-Level Adaptive Trimming for Efficient CoT Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30832)
*Jian Yao,Xiongcai Luo,Ran Cheng,Kay Chen Tan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于理论指导的、分段感知的推理链修剪方法SLAT，以减少大模型在思维链推理中的冗余，显著降低计算开销的同时保持准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的推理链压缩方法通常采用统一的长度惩罚，缺乏对不同推理片段重要性的区分，容易误删有用信息；而实际发现冗余主要集中在高概率但低边际效用的片段中，因此需要更细粒度的控制机制。

Method: 通过理论建模分析推理片段在准确性与长度权衡目标下的次优性，并据此设计了分段级自适应修剪（SLAT）强化学习框架，实现对低效推理片段的选择性抑制。

Result: 在标准基准上的实验表明，SLAT相比未压缩基线可将推理长度减少50%，同时保持具有竞争力的准确性，实现了更优的精度-效率帕累托前沿。

Conclusion: 理论驱动的、分段感知的修剪策略是提升大语言模型推理效率的一个有前景的方向。

Abstract: Recent advances in Large Reasoning Models have significantly improved chain-of-thought (CoT) capabilities via reinforcement learning (RL). However, generated reasoning chains frequently suffer from structural redundancy (i.e., \emph{overthinking}), incurring high computational overhead without improving answer correctness. Existing mitigation strategies typically rely on token-uniform length penalties, which provide coarse, segment-agnostic pressure toward shorter outputs and can inadvertently suppress useful reasoning alongside redundancy. To address this, we demonstrate that inefficiency concentrates in high-probability segments with low marginal utility. We derive a theoretical characterization of segment suboptimality under the correctness-length trade-off objective and propose \textsc{SLAT} (Segment-Level Adaptive Trimming), an RL framework that selectively suppresses redundant segments based on this criterion. Empirical results on standard benchmarks indicate that \textsc{SLAT} establishes a superior accuracy-efficiency Pareto frontier, reducing reasoning length by $50\%$ relative to uncompressed baselines while maintaining competitive accuracy. Overall, our results suggest that theoretically grounded, segment-aware trimming is a promising direction for efficient CoT reasoning in large language models.

</details>


### [111] [Distilling LLM Feedback for Lean Theorem Proving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30861)
*Gaetan Narozniak,Gérard Biau,Rémi Munos,Ahmad Rammal,Pierre Marion*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为Feedback Distillation的训练方法，用于改进推理模型的后训练过程，该方法通过在token级别上利用特权反馈进行自我蒸馏，解决了传统GRPO算法存在的稀疏奖励、探索不足和模式崩溃问题，并在Lean4定理证明任务中表现出更高的策略熵和更好的性能扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决GRPO在推理模型后训练中存在的稀疏奖励、有限探索和模式崩溃问题，同时提升生成轨迹的多样性与性能。

Method: 提出Feedback Distillation方法，使模型在token级别上学习匹配自身在特权反馈条件下的输出分布，结合自蒸馏机制并引入外部知识以提供更密集的监督信号。

Result: 在Lean4定理证明任务中，Feedback Distillation相比GRPO能维持更高的生成多样性（更高策略熵）和更优的pass@k扩展性；且两者结合使用时性能进一步提升。

Conclusion: Feedback Distillation为复杂推理任务的后训练提供了一条有前景的改进路径，且可与强化学习方法协同增效。

Abstract: Post-training for reasoning models typically combines supervised fine-tuning with reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards, most commonly with GRPO. However, this algorithm suffers from sparse rewards, limited exploration, and mode collapse. Building upon recent works on self-distillation, we propose Feedback Distillation, a training method where the model is trained to match, at the token level, its own distribution conditioned on privileged feedback produced by a language model. Feedback Distillation offers token-level supervision and can inject external knowledge. Evaluating our method for Lean4 theorem-proving, we find that Feedback Distillation maintains greater diversity in generated trajectories than GRPO, yielding higher policy entropy and better pass@k scaling. The two methods are complementary: initializing GRPO from a Feedback Distillation checkpoint outperforms either method alone. All in all, our results suggest a promising avenue to improve post-training for complex reasoning.

</details>


### [112] [UniScale: Adaptive Unified Inference Scaling via Online Joint Optimization of Model Routing and Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30898)
*Kaiyu Huang,Xingyu Wang,Mingze Kong,Zhubo Shi,Yuqian Hou,Hong Xu,Zhongxiang Dai,Minchen Yu,Qingjiang Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了统一推理扩展（Unified Inference Scaling, UIS），将模型路由与测试时扩展结合，以优化大语言模型推理中的质量与计算成本权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在模型路由和测试时扩展上分开处理，导致性能调整粗糙且存在计算瓶颈，难以适应动态推理需求。

Method: 提出UniScale框架，将自适应UIS建模为上下文多臂赌博机问题，采用LinUCB算法进行在线学习，并引入效率感知学习与成本建模以优化高维动作空间。

Result: 实验表明，UniScale能在多种动态推理场景中实现更细粒度、更稳定的质量-成本权衡，显著优于分离式方法。

Conclusion: 统一模型路由与测试时扩展可突破传统方法局限，为大模型推理提供更灵活高效的解决方案。

Abstract: In real-world deployments of large language models (LLMs), balancing inference quality and computational cost has become a central challenge. Existing approaches tackle this trade-off along two largely independent dimensions: model routing, which switches among models of different scales to match request complexity, and test-time scaling (TTS), which adjusts inference-time compute within a fixed model for fine-grained control. However, this decoupled design introduces inherent limitations. Model routing yields coarse-grained, discrete performance changes due to the sparse set of model scales, while single-model TTS often encounters capacity ceilings and exhibits diminishing returns as compute increases. Moreover, treating the two mechanisms separately restricts adaptability in dynamic inference environments. To overcome these limitations, we introduce Unified Inference Scaling (UIS), which unifies model routing and TTS in a single optimization space. Building on this formulation, we propose UniScale, an online framework that models adaptive UIS as a contextual multi-armed bandit problem and learns inference policies via LinUCB. The framework incorporates efficiency-aware learning and cost modeling to ensure stable and scalable optimization over high-dimensional action spaces. Evaluation shows that UniScale effectively exploits the synergy in the UIS space to deliver a fine-grained and consistently better quality-cost trade-off across diverse, dynamic inference scenarios.

</details>


### [113] [BilliardPhys-Bench: Benchmarking Physical Reasoning and Visual Dynamics of Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30900)
*Ben Wang,Xiaogang Li,Ruochen Gao,Peiyao Xiao,Chengliang Xu,Zeyu Wang,Zichao Chen,Bing Zhao,Hu Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了BilliardPhys-Bench，一个用于评估合成台球环境中物理推理能力的基准测试，揭示了当前多模态大语言模型在处理视觉动态和物理交互时的局限性，尤其是存在“静止偏差”问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的多模态模型在静态图像识别上表现良好，但在直观物理推理方面仍较弱，难以从单张图像预测物体的运动与交互。因此需要一个专门的基准来评估模型在物理动态场景中的推理能力。

Method: 提出BilliardPhys-Bench，使用程序化引擎生成包含摩擦力和弹性碰撞的随机台球场景，测试模型在球体碰撞、墙壁反弹和最终位置估计三方面的能力，并对GPT、Claude、Gemini和Qwen系列的多模态大语言模型进行评估。

Result: 随着模拟时间增加和场景几何复杂度提高，模型性能下降；发现一种称为“stasis bias（静止偏差）”的一致性失败模式，即当正确物理结果难以推断时，模型倾向于预测无交互。

Conclusion: 当前多模态大语言模型在视觉动态理解方面存在明显不足，需引入更强的物理归纳偏置以提升其物理推理能力。

Abstract: Current multimodal models handle static image recognition well, but intuitive physical reasoning remains a weakness. Predicting how objects will move and interact from a single image is still difficult for these systems. We present BilliardPhys-Bench, a benchmark for physical reasoning in synthetic billiards environments. Its procedural engine generates randomized scenarios with friction and elastic collisions. The benchmark tests three abilities: (1) predicting ball-to-ball collisions, (2) reasoning about wall bounces, and (3) estimating final ball positions after motion stops. We evaluate recent MLLMs from the GPT, Claude, Gemini, and Qwen families. Performance drops as simulation time increases and scene geometry grows more complex. We also observe a consistent failure mode we call "stasis bias": when the correct physical outcome is harder to infer, models tend to predict no interaction. These findings show where current MLLMs break down on visual dynamics and point toward the need for better physical inductive biases in multimodal architectures.

</details>


### [114] [A Persona-Based Evaluation Framework for Pluralistic Alignment in Generative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31021)
*Atahan Karagoz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于状态空间约束的AI评估框架，通过模拟多样化的人类认知角色实现多元化的评估方式，但发现其在序列推理中存在一致性退化问题，需引入动态调节机制以维持稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI对齐方法依赖单一基准测试，忽视了人类判断的文化、人口和情境多样性，无法真实反映现实世界中的评价差异。

Method: 构建一个受状态空间约束的模拟框架，用合成的认知角色（persona）替代单一评估函数，在潜变量流形上建模生成式AI的多视角评估能力，并分析其在顺序推理与随机提示扰动下的稳定性。

Result: 现代生成模型能一致地实例化和维持多种评估角色，实现更贴近真实共识变异性的评估；但在长期推理中出现角色连贯性退化，表现为状态空间漂移和语义不一致。

Conclusion: 静态对齐约束不足以维持长期稳定的评估行为，需在生成系统中嵌入动态的、以生存性为导向的调控机制，为更自适应、人性化的AI评估提供基础。

Abstract: Current alignment paradigms for generative artificial intelligence rely predominantly on monolithic benchmarking frameworks that reduce the plurality of human judgment to aggregated statistical baselines, thereby obscuring cultural, demographic, and contextual variability in evaluation. We introduce a state-space constrained emulation framework for AI evaluation that replaces singular assessment functions with a structured manifold of synthetic cognitive profiles representing diverse human perspectives. We show that modern generative architectures can instantiate and maintain these evaluative personas with high consistency, enabling a form of pluralistic, perspective-dependent benchmarking that more closely reflects real-world consensus variability. However, we further analyze the stability of these simulated evaluators under sequential inference and stochastic prompt perturbations, revealing systematic degradation in persona coherence that manifests as state-space drift and semantic inconsistency. These findings suggest that static alignment constraints are insufficient for sustaining robust evaluative behavior over time.
