Instead, we argue for the necessity of embedding dynamic, viability-driven regulatory mechanisms within generative systems to preserve coherent cognitive emulation. By framing persona-based evaluation as a structured dynamical system over latent representation manifolds, this study provides a foundation for more adaptive, human-aligned, and context-sensitive approaches to AI evaluation.

</details>


### [115] [HADT: A Heterogeneous Multi-Agent Differential Transformer for Autonomous Earth Observation Satellite Cluster](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31023)
*Mohamad A. Hady,Muhammad Anwar Masum,Siyi Hu,Mahardhika Pratama,Jimmy Cao,Ryszard Kowalczyk*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Transformer的新型架构，用于异构卫星集群在地球观测任务中的自主资源管理，通过关系型观测-动作标记化和差异注意力机制，实现了优于现有基线方法的性能，并展现出良好的适应性和可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于数学模型的调度方法在面对动态变化和不确定性的空间任务环境时效果有限，难以满足异构卫星集群实时、自适应资源管理的需求。

Method: 将资源管理问题重构为序列决策过程，采用无需依赖环境模型的强化学习技术，设计了一种新型的Transformer架构，引入关系型观测-动作标记化和差异注意力机制，以提升在异构卫星集群中的决策能力。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提方法在性能上显著优于现有基线方法，并且对不同规模的卫星集群表现出强适应性和可转移性。

Conclusion: 该方法为异构卫星集群在复杂动态环境下实现自主、实时的资源管理提供了有效解决方案，具有良好的应用前景。

Abstract: This work addresses the problem of autonomous resource management in heterogeneous satellite cluster conducting Earth Observation (EO) missions including optical and Synthetic Aperture Radar (SAR) satellites. In autonomous operation mode, satellites are equipped with intelligent capabilities enabling real-time decision-making based on the latest conditions, while requiring minimal interaction with ground operators. Traditional scheduling approaches typically rely on mathematical models to represent satellite mission and resource management. Then, this problem is solved by using optimization algorithms. However, such solutions become less effective when the underlying models are not available, over complex, and inaccurate due to dynamic changes and uncertainties inherent in the space mission environment. A promising alternative is to reformulate the problem as a sequential decision-making process and apply model-free reinforcement learning techniques to enable adaptive and real-time resource management. To this end, we propose a novel transformer-based architecture tailored for heterogeneous satellite cluster autonomous EO Mission with relational observations-actions tokenization and differential attention mechanism. Our experimental results demonstrate significant performance improvements compared to the available baselines. Moreover, the proposed architecture exhibits strong adaptability and transferability with respect to varying numbers of satellite clusters.

</details>


### [116] [GraphARC: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Graph-Based Abstract Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31031)
*Saku Peltonen,August Bøgh Rønberg,Andreas Plesner,Roger Wattenhofer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GraphARC是一个用于图结构数据上抽象推理的基准，扩展了ARC的少样本转换学习范式，支持系统评估模型在局部、全局和层次化图变换任务上的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有抽象推理基准多局限于网格或文本格式，缺乏对图结构数据的系统性评估，而关系推理是智能的核心，因此需要一个面向图结构的抽象推理基准。

Method: 提出GraphARC基准，将ARC的少样本转换学习推广到图结构数据，每个任务需从少量输入-输出对中推断变换规则，并应用于新的测试图，涵盖多种图变换类型，并可跨不同图族和规模大规模生成实例。

Result: 在GraphARC上评估了当前主流语言模型，发现它们虽能回答图属性问题，但在完整图变换任务上表现不佳，显示出理解与执行之间的差距，且在更大规模实例上性能进一步下降。

Conclusion: GraphARC为评估模型的图推理能力提供了一个具有挑战性的新基准，揭示了现有模型在图转换任务中的局限性，也为未来图基础模型的发展提供了统一的测试平台。

Abstract: Relational reasoning lies at the heart of intelligence, but existing benchmarks are typically confined to formats such as grids or text. We introduce GraphARC, a benchmark for abstract reasoning on graph-structured data. GraphARC generalizes the few-shot transformation learning paradigm of the Abstraction and Reasoning Corpus (ARC). Each task requires inferring a transformation rule from a few input-output pairs and applying it to a new test graph, covering local, global, and hierarchical graph transformations. Unlike grid-based ARC, GraphARC instances can be generated at scale across diverse graph families and sizes, enabling systematic evaluation of generalization abilities. We evaluate state-of-the-art language models on GraphARC and observe clear limitations. Models can answer questions about graph properties but often fail to solve the full graph transformation task, revealing a comprehension-execution gap. Performance further degrades on larger instances, exposing scaling barriers. More broadly, by combining aspects of node classification, link prediction, and graph generation within a single framework, GraphARC provides a promising testbed for future graph foundation models.

</details>


### [117] [Vector Linking via Cross-Model Local Isometric Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31100)
*Ziying Chen,Yang Cao,He Sun,Beining Yang,Tianjian Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了向量链接问题，即在两个由不同黑盒编码器生成的部分重叠数据集的嵌入云之间，仅使用向量恢复跨模型对象对应关系。作者发现独立训练的对比编码器表现出局部几何一致性，并据此提出一种基于参考的迭代几何嵌入哈希方法，利用少量配对锚点恢复向量链接。实验表明该方法在多种基准和模型组合下具有高准确性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决不同黑盒编码器生成的嵌入空间中对象对应关系难以恢复的问题，尤其是在缺乏共享输入或模型结构信息的情况下，实现跨模型语义对齐与数据集成。

Method: 利用对比编码器的局部几何一致性，通过距离尺度不变性构建哈希表示；采用迭代方式，基于少量初始配对锚点匹配哈希空间候选链接，并利用Beta-Bernoulli后验聚合多视角证据，逐步扩展高置信度链接作为新锚点。

Result: 在多个基准和嵌入模型组合上验证了方法的有效性，能够在不同重叠程度、种子预算和域外锚点情况下实现准确且鲁棒的向量链接。

Conclusion: 独立训练的对比编码器虽存在全局失真，但保留局部几何结构，可被用于无须共享输入的跨模型向量链接，所提方法为向量数据库整合与跨模型聚类提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We study Vector Linking: given two embedding clouds produced by different black-box encoders over partially overlapping datasets, recover cross-model object correspondences using only vectors. Empirically and theoretically, we show that independently trained contrastive encoders exhibit local geometric consistency: short-range distances are approximately preserved up to a scale factor, while long-range distances are not due to model-specific distortion. Building on this, we propose an iterative, reference-based geometric embedding hashing that recovers vector links from a tiny seed set of paired anchors. It represents each vector by distances to sampled paired anchors, proposes candidate links via hash-space matching, and aggregates evidence across views in a Beta-Bernoulli posterior to bootstrap high-confidence links as new anchors. Experiments across multiple benchmarks and embedding model pairs demonstrate accurate and robust linking under varying overlap, seed budgets, and out-of-domain anchors, with applications to vector database integration and cross-model clustering. Code is available at https://github.com/DBgroup-Edinburgh/VecLinking.

</details>


### [118] [LLM-FACETS: A Privacy-Preserving Framework for Evaluating LLM Transparency and Accountability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31167)
*Tom Lucas,Alessio Buscemi,Alfredo Capozucca,German Castignani,Barbara Delacroix*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLM-FACETS是一个开源框架，旨在让非技术从业者也能参与大语言模型的审计，通过浏览器界面和插件架构支持事实性、认知校准和可重复性评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM评估工具需要编程技能或依赖外部云平台，限制了领域专家和合规官员的参与，且存在数据隐私风险。

Method: 设计了一个基于三种用户角色（技术专家、领域专家、合规官员）的系统架构，内置确定性指标（如BLEU、ROUGE）、LLM裁判指标，并提供日志概率可视化、多裁判共识和RAG Triad指标；所有数据流明确分离，支持自托管和插件扩展。

Result: 实现了18个指标与标准库的交叉验证，验证了框架的准确性；支持跨指标交叉核验，提升透明度与可复现性。

Conclusion: LLM-FACETS降低了AI审计门槛，增强了透明性和可控性，有助于实现负责任的AI部署，尤其符合欧盟AI法案和NIST风险管理框架的要求。

Abstract: Assessing whether Large Language Models outputs are factually grounded, epistemically calibrated, and methodologically reproducible is a prerequisite for responsible AI deployment. Yet auditing LLMs remains inaccessible to non-technical practitioners: existing tools require programming expertise and non-trivial environment setup, and cloud-hosted platforms transmit evaluation data to external services, creating barriers for domain experts and compliance officers legally responsible for AI oversight. We introduce LLM-FACETS (LLM FActuality Cross-EvaluaTion System): an open-source framework with a browser-accessible interface and a plugin architecture, structured around three practitioner profiles (technical experts, domain experts, compliance officers) that mirror the stakeholder categories identified in the EU AI Act and the NIST AI Risk Management Framework. The architecture makes data flows explicit: deterministic metrics (BLEU, ROUGE, BERTScore) run entirely within the self-hosted server with no outbound transmission; LLM-judge metrics contact external APIs explicitly, with users retaining full credential control. The framework operationalizes transparency through three mechanisms: token-level log-probability visualization for epistemic uncertainty, multi-judge consensus to mitigate judge bias, and RAG Triad metrics (Faithfulness, Answer Relevance, Context Relevance) to detect and localize hallucinations. A plugin architecture allows any new metric or dataset to be integrated without modifying the evaluation pipeline. The open-source implementation enables cross-checking across multiple metrics targeting the same property, ensuring reproducibility and decoupling AI accountability from the teams building the systems assessed. We verify the framework through cross-validation of 18 metric implementations against canonical reference libraries.

</details>


### [119] [Formalizing and falsifying causal pathways of rare events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31254)
*Anahita Haghighat,Dominik Janzing*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出因果路径的正式定义，探讨其可测试含义，并引入稀有事件因果路径的抽象，连接了简单的因果解释与详细的因果建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于对结构方程模型中罕见事件（异常值）的根因分析的近期形式化工作，希望定义因果路径并明确其在实际中的可检验性。

Method: 通过形式化定义因果路径，识别其在仅依赖稀有事件路径因果抽象时的条件，并提出相应的因果结构抽象方法。

Result: 明确了因果路径的可测试含义可以在不依赖完整因果图的情况下，仅通过路径上的因果抽象得出。

Conclusion: 所提出的因果路径抽象能够有效桥接直观的言语因果解释与复杂的因果模型，适用于分析罕见事件的根因。

Abstract: Building on recent formalizations of root cause analysis for rare events (``outliers'') in structural equation models, we propose a formal definition of a causal pathway and discuss its testable implications. We identify conditions under which these implications depend only on a causal abstraction defined by the pathway of rare events, rather than on the full causal graph of the underlying system. Accordingly, we introduce an abstraction of causal structure to pathways of rare events that bridges simple verbal causal explanations and detailed causal modeling.

</details>


### [120] [Learning to Adapt: Self-Improving Web Agent via Cognitive-Aware Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31365)
*Weile Chen,Bingchen Miao,Qifan Yu,Wendong Bu,Guoming Wang,Wenqiao Zhang,Shengyu Zhang,Juncheng Li,Siliang Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为SCALE的自认知学习与探索框架，通过引入三种对抗性角色和图探索策略SCALE-Hop，提升多模态大语言模型在复杂网页环境中的自主性和泛化能力，并构建了大规模数据集SCALE-20k以支持训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网页智能体依赖人工设计的执行流程或高成本专家轨迹，难以适应复杂动态环境，缺乏自主发现自身局限并拓展认知边界的能力。

Method: 提出SCALE框架，包含Selector、Predictor和Judger三个对抗性角色，用于自我认知与环境探索；设计SCALE-Hop图探索策略以实现全局规划；构建SCALE-20k数据集，基于真实网站任务和结构化示范支持模型学习。

Result: 实验表明，该方法显著提升了多种多模态大语言模型在不同网页环境下的性能和泛化能力，在任务完成率和探索效率方面优于基线方法。

Conclusion: SCALE提供了一个可扩展且通用的解决方案，推动了真正自主、自适应网页智能体的发展，为未来无需人工干预的智能体训练奠定了基础。

Abstract: Recent advances in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have led to promising progress in web agents. However, existing web agents often rely on handcrafted execution pipelines or expensive expert trajectories, limiting their adaptability to complex, dynamic environments. To address these challenges, we propose SCALE (Self-Cognitive-Aware Learning and Exploration), which leverages three adversarial roles, Selector, Predictor, and Judger to autonomously discover the agent's limitations and expand its cognitive boundaries through environmental exploration. Moreover, we propose SCALE-Hop, a graph exploration strategy that facilitates global planning and helps agents avoid local exploration traps. To further support learning, we construct SCALE-20k, a large-scale dataset collected from 19 real-world websites, containing diverse task types and structured demonstrations generated from SCALE's exploration traces. Experimental results show that our approach significantly improves the performance and generalization of multiple MLLMs in various web environments. Our framework offers a scalable and generalizable solution for building truly autonomous and adaptive web agents.

</details>


### [121] [HypoAgent: An Agentic Framework for Interactive Abductive Hypothesis Generation over Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31370)
*Yisen Gao,Yixi Cai,Tianshi Zheng,Jiaxin Bai,Yangqiu Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出HypoAgent，一个用于知识图谱上交互式溯因假设生成的智能体框架，通过整合意图识别、假设生成和根本原因分析三个智能体，提升多轮对话中的假设生成效果与可诊断性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可控假设生成方法在多轮对话中难以处理动态变化的自然语言意图，且缺乏对生成失败的细粒度诊断能力。

Method: 设计HypoAgent框架，包含三个智能体：意图识别智能体将用户话语和对话历史转化为知识图谱可执行条件；假设生成智能体根据提取的意图进行可控假设生成；根本原因分析智能体通过KG邻域探测诊断不可靠假设并提供修正建议。

Result: 在常识和生物医学领域的知识图谱上实验表明，HypoAgent在单轮、多轮及无条件设置下均达到最先进的语义相似度表现。

Conclusion: HypoAgent有效提升了知识图谱上交互式溯因推理的能力，支持动态意图理解和生成结果的可解释性优化。

Abstract: Abductive reasoning over knowledge graphs aims to generate logical hypotheses that explain observed entities or facts. Existing controllable hypothesis generation methods allow users to guide this process with explicit conditions, but they remain limited in interactive settings: they struggle to ground evolving natural-language intents across multi-turn dialogues and provide little fine-grained diagnosis when generated hypotheses fail. To address these limitations, we propose HypoAgent, an Agentic framework for interactive abductive Hypothesis Generation over knowledge graphs. HypoAgent integrates three agents: an Intent Recognition Agent that grounds user utterances and dialogue history into executable KG conditions, a Hypothesis Generation Agent that performs controllable hypothesis generation according to the extracted user intention, and a Root Cause Analysis Agent that diagnoses unreliable hypothesis fragments and leverages KG neighborhood probing to identify supported refinements. Experiments on commonsense and biomedical domain-specific knowledge graphs demonstrate that HypoAgent achieves state-of-the-art semantic similarity under single-turn, multi-turn, and unconditional settings. Our code is available at https://github.com/HKUST-KnowComp/HypoAgent.

</details>


### [122] [FAM-Bench: A Multimodal Benchmark for Condition-Aware Food-as-Medicine Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31410)
*Mingyang Mao,Bhargav Rishi Medisetti,Utkarsh Grover,Tanvir Ibrahim,Wenyan Li,Tingting Zhang,Xiaomin Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FAM-Bench是一个新的多模态食品即药物基准测试，包含2500个经营养专家验证的实例，用于评估模型在13种饮食相关健康状况下对食物适宜性的判断能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有食品AI基准主要关注菜肴识别、食谱理解或营养估算，缺乏对特定健康条件下食物选择是否合适的决策能力评估。

Method: 提出FAM-Bench，包含两个任务：基于图像和成分列表判断菜肴是否适合特定健康状况的适宜性评估，以及对四个候选菜肴按适宜性排序的比较分析。

Result: 该基准测试要求模型整合成分信息、视觉准备线索和临床营养限制，为语言和视觉-语言模型提供标准化的健康感知推理测试平台。

Conclusion: FAM-Bench填补了健康感知食物决策评估的空白，推动食品AI向医疗应用发展。

Abstract: Food-as-Medicine requires models to reason beyond what a dish is or what nutrition it contains: they must decide whether a concrete food choice is appropriate for a specific health condition. Existing food AI benchmarks primarily evaluate dish recognition, recipe understanding, nutrient estimation, or general nutrition question answering, leaving this health-aware decision layer largely untested. We introduce FAM-Bench, a multi-modal Food-as-Medicine benchmark with 2500 nutrition-expert-verified instances across 13 diet-related health conditions. The benchmark contains two complementary tasks: dish-level suitability assessment, where models judge whether a dish is suitable for a condition from its image and ingredient list, and comparative dish analysis, where models rank four candidate dishes by condition-specific suitability. Both tasks require integrating ingredient evidence, visual preparation cues, and clinical nutrition constraints, providing a standardized testbed for grounded health-aware reasoning in language and vision-language models.

</details>


### [123] [Answer-Set-Programming-based Abstractions for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31444)
*Rafael Bankosegger,Thomas Eiter,Johannes Oetsch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了使用Answer-Set Programming (ASP) 实现CARCASS框架以构建强化学习中的抽象表示，实验结果表明该方法在拥有领域知识时具有潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于现实问题通常涉及巨大的状态空间，使得学习和泛化变得困难，因此需要抽象和近似方法。

Method: 采用Answer-Set Programming (ASP) 这种声明性建模语言来实现CARCASS框架，并在Blocks World和Minigrid两个领域中进行案例研究。

Result: 实验结果显示，结合ASP的CARCASS在构建强化学习的抽象表示方面表现良好，尤其是在有领域知识可用的情况下。

Conclusion: 使用ASP实现的CARCASS为强化学习中的抽象构造提供了一种有前景的方法。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) enables autonomous agents to learn policies from experience, but realistic problems often involve enormous state spaces, making learning and generalisation challenging. Abstraction and approximation are therefore essential. Relational Reinforcement Learning (RRL) offers a way to reason about objects and their relations, and the CARCASS framework by Martijn van Otterlo demonstrates how logical representations can model Markov Decision Processes (MDPs) in first-order domains. Originally implemented in Prolog, CARCASS leverages domain knowledge to create powerful abstractions. We explore Answer-Set Programming (ASP), which is a rich and, contrary to Prolog, fully declarative modelling language, to realise CARCASS abstractions. We evaluate our ASP-based implementation in case studies of two domains, viz. Blocks World and Minigrid. Our results indicate that CARCASS with ASP provides a promising approach to constructing abstractions for RL, especially when domain knowledge is available.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [124] [CoMo3R-SLAM: Collaborative Monocular Dense SLAM with Learned 3D Reconstruction Priors for Outdoor Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30488)
*Zhihao Cao,Qi Shao,Shuhao Zhai,Feng Tian,Anh Nguyen,Hesheng Wang,Baoru Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CoMo3R-SLAM是首个基于单目RGB相机的协作式稠密SLAM系统，利用学习到的前馈3D重建先验实现户外多智能体场景下的可扩展、一致的三维感知，无需深度传感器或参数内参，在多个数据集上达到领先或与RGB-D方法相当的精度，同时以8 FPS在线运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协作式稠密SLAM系统通常依赖深度传感器，带来高负载、功耗和标定成本；而单目相机虽轻便，但存在尺度模糊和跨智能体数据关联不可靠的问题，尤其在重叠区域少、结构重复的户外场景中传统特征匹配效果差，因此需要更鲁棒的几何信息支持。

Method: 提出CoMo3R-SLAM，每个智能体使用先验引导的前端实现实时跟踪与局部稠密融合，协调器通过稠密点图匹配进行跨智能体验证、闭式Sim(3)规范同步，并结合段级深度优化进行GPU加速的全局光束法平差。

Result: 在Tanks and Temples和Waymo数据集上测试显示，该系统在四个Tanks and Temples场景中的三个取得了最优ATE结果，Waymo上的精度具有竞争力，且仅使用单目RGB即可实现鲁棒的跨智能体约束和全局一致的度量地图。

Conclusion: CoMo3R-SLAM首次实现了完全基于单目RGB的协作式稠密SLAM，无需深度输入或相机内参，通过引入学习型3D先验和高效的全局优化策略，在户外多机器人场景中实现了高精度、一致的实时三维重建。

Abstract: Collaborative dense SLAM is essential for multi-robot teams to achieve scalable and consistent 3D perception across large-scale outdoor environments. Existing systems typically depend on depth sensors, incurring significant payload, power, and calibration costs. Monocular RGB cameras are a lightweight alternative, but collaborative monocular dense SLAM remains difficult due to scale ambiguity, unreliable inter-agent data association, especially in outdoor scenes where low overlap and repetitive structures make traditional feature matching unreliable, motivating robust geometric information. We propose CoMo3R-SLAM, the first collaborative monocular dense RGB SLAM system that leverages robust learned feed-forward 3D reconstruction priors for outdoor multi-agent mapping. Each agent runs a prior-guided front-end for real-time tracking and local dense fusion, while a coordinator performs dense pointmap matching for cross-agent verification, closed-form Sim(3) gauge synchronization, and GPU-accelerated global bundle adjustment with segment-level depth optimization. Requiring neither depth sensors nor parametric intrinsics, our system produces robust cross-agent constraints and globally consistent metric maps from monocular RGB alone. On Tanks and Temples and Waymo sequences, CoMo3R-SLAM achieves the best ATE on three of four Tanks and Temples scenes and competitive Waymo accuracy, matching or exceeding state-of-the-art RGB-D methods while running online at 8 FPS.

</details>


### [125] [Physics-informed Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning under Hybrid Contact Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30503)
*Vittorio Giammarino,Anastasios Manganaris,Ahmed H. Qureshi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了物理信息引导的目标条件强化学习（Pi-GCRL）在接触密集型操作任务中性能下降的问题，提出了一种具有接触感知和分层结构的新方法，以更有效地引入物理先验知识，从而提升在复杂动力学环境中的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Pi-GCRL方法在处理高维、混合或依赖接触的动力学系统时表现不佳，尤其在接触丰富的操作任务中因非光滑值函数和模式依赖的可控性而失效，因此需要改进其适用性。

Method: 提出了接触感知和分层的Pi-GCRL框架，选择性地在操作问题的不同部分应用基于最优控制的归纳偏置，以更好地建模接触引起的混合动力学。

Result: 实验表明，新方法在接触丰富的操作任务中显著优于标准Pi-GCRL方法，验证了其在处理复杂接触动态方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过引入结构化的归纳偏置，所提出的分层与接触感知方法为将Pi-GCRL扩展到接触密集型操作任务提供了可靠且原则性的解决方案。

Abstract: Learning to reach arbitrary goals from sparse feedback requires agents to infer a rich notion of reachability across state--goal pairs. Goal-conditioned reinforcement learning (GCRL) tackles this challenge by learning policies that generalize across goals, but this generalization becomes increasingly difficult as the underlying dynamics become high-dimensional, hybrid, or contact-dependent. To address this issue, physics-informed GCRL (Pi-GCRL) introduces optimal-control-inspired inductive biases into goal-conditioned value learning. While Pi-GCRL methods have proven effective in navigation and object-free goal-reaching domains, their reliability in contact-rich tasks remains unclear, where contact interactions induce hybrid dynamics, mode-dependent controllability, and nonsmooth value landscapes. In this work, we show that these structural properties can cause existing Pi-GCRL methods to degrade when applied naively to contact-rich manipulation. Motivated by this analysis, we introduce contact-aware and hierarchical formulations that apply physics-informed inductive biases selectively across the manipulation problem. Our results provide a principled step toward extending Pi-GCRL to contact-rich manipulation.

</details>


### [126] [VLM-GLoc: Vision-Language Model Enhanced Monte Carlo Localization for Robust Semantic Global Localization in Cluttered Quasi-Static Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30506)
*Shivendra Agrawal,Bradley Hayes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VLM-GLoc是一种利用开放词汇视觉-语言模型（VLM）进行分层语义蒙特卡洛定位的方法，通过文本到地图检索和语义观测前端，在几何和语义模糊的室内环境中实现高效全局定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在如杂货店、办公室等具有几何重复性和语义长尾分布的静态环境中，传统定位方法因依赖几何特征或特定视觉管道而难以应对模糊性和动态干扰，因此需要更鲁棒的语义感知定位方案。

Method: 提出VLM-GLoc，采用视觉-语言模型作为统一的语义观测前端，结合分层语义蒙特卡洛定位，并引入逆向语义提议机制，通过文本描述生成粒子初始化位置，实现基于语义的地图匹配与定位。

Result: 在两个不同真实环境（3500平方英尺杂货店和3700平方英尺实验室）及两种平台（手机和四足机器人）上测试，VLM-GLoc分别达到70%和74%的全局定位成功率，显著优于仅几何或领域特定基线方法。

Conclusion: 利用VLM的丰富语义表达能力可有效提升机器人在复杂重复环境中的定位性能，验证了语义先验与逆向提议机制在全局定位中的有效性。

Abstract: Global localization in geometrically aliased, quasi-static environments such as grocery stores, offices, schools, and hospitals poses a significant challenge for mobile robots. Grocery stores with parallel aisles and a long tailed distribution of products, as well as offices and labs with repetitive furniture such as chairs, desks, monitors, and doors, exemplify common indoor environments that present geometric and even semantic ambiguity. Traditional approaches rely either on distinct geometric features or on domain-specific vision pipelines that struggle with long-tail semantic distributions and transient visual clutter. We present VLM-GLoc, a method for hierarchical semantic Monte Carlo Localization (MCL) that leverages open-vocabulary Vision-Language Models (VLMs) as a unified semantic observation front-end. We hypothesize a three-fold benefit from VLMs: (1) extracting highly discriminative rich text features, (2) implicit quality filtering of blurry or dynamic objects, and (3) permanence reasoning for targeted data augmentation. We introduce an inverse semantic proposal mechanism that seeds particles via text-to-map retrieval. Evaluated across two real-world environments with different characteristics and two different platforms: a 3,500 sq. ft. grocery store with a cellphone and a 3,700 sq. ft. lab space with a quadruped, VLM-GLoc achieves 70% and 74% global localization success respectively, substantially outperforming traditional geometry-only and domain-specific baselines.

</details>


### [127] [ARISTO Hand: Sensing-Driven Distal Hyperextension for Fine-Grained Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30508)
*Aaron Kim,Dong Ho Kang,Mark Helwig,Mingyo Seo,Kazuto Yokoyama,Tetsuya Narita,Luis Sentis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ARISTO Hand是一种集成主动远端超伸展和混合指尖感知架构的腱驱动机器人手，可显著提升对薄物体的操作能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多仿人机器人手缺乏精确操控细小物体所需的接触几何精度和力感知能力。

Method: 设计了一种具有主动远端超伸展功能的腱驱动机器人手，并结合刚性指甲安装式力矩传感器与柔性电容触觉阵列的混合感知架构。

Result: 主动超伸展使1-20mm厚度物体的拔出力提高2.76倍；刚性传感器在边缘接触时提供可靠的力测量，验证了该架构在SD卡插拔任务中的有效性。

Conclusion: ARISTO Hand通过结构与感知创新，显著提升了机器人对薄物体的精细操作性能。

Abstract: Manipulating thin objects requires precise contact geometry and reliable force perception, yet many anthropomorphic robotic hands lack the mechanical and sensing capabilities needed for such interactions. We present the ARISTO Hand, a tendon-driven robotic hand that integrates active distal hyperextension with a hybrid fingertip-sensing architecture that combines a rigid, nail-mounted force-torque sensor and a soft capacitive tactile array. Active hyperextension enables controlled fingertip engagement beyond the kinematic limits of standard flexion, increasing pull-out force by 2.76x for object thicknesses of 1-20 mm while preserving the nominal grasp capability. The rigid nail-mounted sensor provides reliable force measurements during edge contacts, where the sensitivity of proprioceptive force estimation degrades as the contact geometry approaches kinematic singularities. We validate the proposed architecture through quantitative force characterization and a multi-stage SD card extraction and insertion task. Video and supplementary materials are available at: https://aristohand.github.io

</details>


### [128] [Any-ttach: Quick End-effector Swapping Enables Manipulation Dexterity with Simplicity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30569)
*Weizhe Ni,Jinzhou Li,Haoyu Li,Cody Andres Alessio-Bunnell,Wenjing Pan,Xianyi Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Any-ttach是一个以工具为中心的机器人操作框架，通过快速更换末端执行器来实现灵巧而简单的操作，支持多种工具和模块，并在长周期任务中展示了高效的工具切换与技能执行能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受人类手部复杂性主要用于工具使用和制造的启发，研究者希望探索通过简单机械手配合快速更换末端执行器的方式实现高灵巧性操作，而非依赖高自由度复杂设计。

Method: 提出Any-ttach框架，包含低成本自动换装机构、用于采集人类演示的手持设备，以及融合学习、参数化和规划的工具使用技能的任务规划系统，支持多种工具和末端模块通过统一接口切换。

Result: 实验表明Any-ttach提高了工具更换可靠性，提升了演示效率，降低了工具位姿误差，并成功在制作三明治和准备黄瓜两个长周期任务中执行了六种工具使用子技能。

Conclusion: 机器人不仅可以依靠更复杂的末端执行器提升操作能力，还可以通过快速更换工具和模块来扩展功能，Any-ttach为简化机器人设计同时增强操作灵活性提供了新路径。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation dexterity is often pursued by building increasingly complex high-DoF multifingered hands. While many robotic hands are designed to replicate human morphology, the functional role of human hands suggests a different perspective: much of their complexity may exist to enable tool use and tool making. This observation motivates Any-ttach, a tool-centric manipulation framework that treats quick end-effector swapping as a mechanism for dexterity with simplicity. Any-ttach combines a low-cost automatic swapping mechanism for an open-close robot interface, a handheld device for collecting human demonstrations, and a task planning framework that composes learned, parameterized, and planned tool-use skills. The system supports diverse tools and end-effector modules, including daily tools, articulated tools such as scissors, Fin Ray fingers, and a low-cost anthropomorphic hand, through the same shared interface. Our experiments show that Any-ttach improves tool-swapping reliability, increases demonstration efficiency, reduces tool-pose variability, and supports diverse tool-use skills. In two long-horizon tasks, making a sandwich and preparing a cucumber, Any-ttach executes six tool-use subskills through end-effector switching and execution monitoring. These results suggest that robots can expand manipulation capability not only through more complex end-effectors, but also through rapidly exchangeable tools and end-effector modules. More details and videos are available at https://any-ttach.github.io/.

</details>


### [129] [Caspar: CUDA Accelerator for Symbolic Programming with Adaptive Reordering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30583)
*Emil Martens,Aaron Miller,Matias Varnum,Annette Stahl*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Caspar是一个库，通过自动生成优化的CUDA内核，将现代GPU的强大功能更便捷地引入机器人领域，并展示了在大规模束调整数据集上的卓越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了让机器人领域更方便地利用现代GPU的高性能计算能力，解决现有工具在表达性和性能之间的差距。

Method: 基于SymForce库，通过符号表达式自动生成优化的CUDA内核，并利用符号微分实现非线性优化求解器。

Result: 在BAL数据集上的束调整任务中，Caspar比现有最先进的方法快5到20倍，内存占用更低，且精度相当。

Conclusion: Caspar通过结合符号编程与GPU高性能计算，显著提升了机器人中非线性优化的效率，验证了其方法的有效性和广泛适用性。

Abstract: We present Caspar, a library that makes the power of modern GPUs more accessible in robotics and provides a state-of-the-art nonlinear GPU solver that can be applied to a wide range of different optimization problems. Caspar bridges the gap between expressive symbolic programming in Python and high-performance GPU runtimes in C++ by automatically generating optimized CUDA kernels from symbolic expressions. Building on the SymForce library, users can easily define and combine symbolic expressions, including Lie group operations, to generate custom CUDA kernels. To use Caspar as a solver, users need only define the symbolic residual functions; Caspar then uses symbolic differentiation to generate the necessary GPU kernels and interfaces to perform nonlinear optimization.
  In this paper, we present the core components of Caspar and showcase its performance by performing bundle adjustment on the Bundle Adjustment in the Large (BAL) dataset. We benchmark Caspar against other state-of-the-art bundle adjusters and show that it is 5 to 20 times faster than the best alternative, requires less memory, and achieves similar accuracy. This illustrates the benefit of our symbolic GPU programming approach. Caspar is released as part of SymForce and is freely available at https://github.com/symforce-org/symforce

</details>


### [130] [Exploiting Chordal Sparsity for Globally Optimal Estimation with Factor Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30617)
*Avinash Subramanian,Connor Holmes,Timothy D. Barfoot,Frank Dellaert,Frederike Dümbgen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在GTSAM框架内自动构建因子图凸半定规划（SDP）松弛的新方法，并利用Bayes树结构对SDP问题进行分解，显著提升了求解效率，实现了鲁棒且高效的全局状态估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的状态估计求解器为局部优化方法，容易陷入较差的局部极小值，存在安全隐患；而基于凸松弛的全局方法虽然能提供全局最优解或验证，但通常难以构造且计算成本高昂。因此需要一种自动化、高效且可扩展的全局求解方案。

Method: 在GTSAM框架中设计了一种自动生成常见因子图类型SDP松弛的方法，并结合GTSAM原生的Bayes树结构对SDP问题进行分解，利用弦稀疏性加速求解过程。

Result: 该方法能够自动处理多种因子图结构，在3D位姿图SLAM和2D定位两个案例中表现出优于传统局部求解器的可扩展性，显著缩短了全局求解时间。

Conclusion: 通过结合自动SDP松弛构造与Bayes树结构分解，所提方法实现了高效、鲁棒的全局状态估计，为机器人状态估计提供了实用的全局优化工具。

Abstract: Robust and efficient state estimation is crucial for perception, navigation, and control in robotics. State estimation problems are conveniently modeled using the factor-graph framework as enabled by modern software packages such as GTSAM or g2o. However, the standard solvers included in such frameworks are local and may converge to poor local minima, posing significant safety concerns. Conversely, techniques based on convex relaxations have been shown to provide a means of globally solving or certifying many state estimation problems. However, these relaxations 1) often require substantial effort to formulate, and 2) may incur significantly higher cost compared to efficient local solvers, as they require solving a large semidefinite program (SDP). In this work, we address both shortcomings by 1) creating a new procedure within the GTSAM framework for automatically constructing convex SDP relaxations for any factor graphs with common factor and variable types, and by 2) exploiting the Bayes tree constructions native to GTSAM to decompose the SDP problem, leading to significant speedup in solver time for chordally sparse problems. We demonstrate the favorable scaling of this structure-exploiting global estimator compared to standard local solvers for two case studies: A 3D pose-graph SLAM problem with a ring factor graph and a 2D localization problem with a chain factor graph. The software framework is available at https://github.com/borglab/gtsam.

</details>


### [131] [Bidirectional Incremental Generalized Hybrid A*](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30647)
*Sidharth Talia,Oren Salzman,Siddhartha Srinivasa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Bi-IGHA*的双向增量广义Hybrid A*算法，用于解决复杂动力学系统在非结构化环境中的高效任意时间运动规划问题，通过缓解冻结顶点对搜索路径的遮挡，显著减少了搜索扩展次数，同时保持了算法的收敛性和成本单调优化保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对传统A*算法因维度灾难在高维状态空间中计算不可行，以及Hybrid A*等方法因离散化导致搜索与分辨率耦合的问题，现有方法如IGHA*虽通过多分辨率层次搜索实现任意时间规划，但其冻结顶点机制可能遮挡潜在解路径，限制搜索效率。因此，需要一种能进一步缓解该问题并提升搜索效率的新方法。

Method: 本文提出Bi-IGHA*，将IGHA*扩展至双向搜索框架，在前向（初始状态）和反向（目标状态）同时进行多分辨率搜索，并通过冻结顶点共享与交叉验证机制减少因单向冻结导致的解路径遗漏。该方法保留了IGHA*的迭代优化特性，并利用双向搜索降低有效搜索深度，缓解冻结顶点的负面影响。

Result: 实验结果表明，Bi-IGHA*在R3、R4和R6规划问题上显著减少了节点扩展数量，相比IGHA*在相同闭环性能下实现了更高效的规划，尤其在高速越野自主驾驶任务中表现出更优的计算效率。

Conclusion: Bi-IGHA*通过引入双向搜索机制，有效缓解了IGHA*中冻结顶点遮挡解路径的问题，在保持原有理论保证的同时，显著提升了搜索效率，为复杂动力学系统的任意时间运动规划提供了更高效的解决方案。

Abstract: We focus on the problem of efficient anytime kinodynamic planning for systems with complex dynamics in unstructured environments that make precomputing motion primitives infeasible. Directly applying A* to such problems is computationally infeasible due to the curse of dimensionality. Methods such as Hybrid A* addressed this burden by discretizing the state space, but in turn creating a coupling between tree discovery and the discretization resolution. The Incremental Generalized Hybrid A* (IGHA*) performs search over a hierarchy of resolutions in an anytime fashion to break this coupling, by freezing vertices to use in later search iterations rather than pruning them. However, the frozen vertices can hide solution-supporting vertices from the search at a particular iteration. While classical bidirectional search is motivated by the reduction of search depth, extending IGHA* into the bidirectional setting (termed Bi-IGHA*) obtains additional benefit by fundamentally mitigating the behaviour induced by frozen vertices hiding solutions. We show that Bi-IGHA* preserves IGHA*'s guarantees on monotonic cost improvement and termination. We empirically show that Bi-IGHA* substantially reduces expansions on R3, R4, and R6 planning problems, and achieves equivalent closed-loop performance with kinodynamic planning for high-speed off-road autonomy while requiring significantly fewer expansions. Website: https://personalrobotics.github.io/IGHAStar/biighastar.html

</details>


### [132] [Primitive Subspaces Mediate Few-Shot Transfer in VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30695)
*Anya Singh,Cabrel Happi,Jai Relan,Varun Nair,Vidyut Baradwaj*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了通过引入原始动作感知训练来提升视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略在工业环境中对新任务的少样本迁移能力，发现基于原始动作分割的训练显著提高了样本效率，并验证了其跨架构、数据集和随机种子的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLA模型在部署新任务时需要昂贵的微调，缺乏低成本适应能力。本文旨在探索是否可以通过学习可组合的子技能库，实现无需微调即可快速适应新任务的目标。

Method: 作者在REASSEMBLE数据集上训练两种VLA架构（OpenVLA和$π_{0.5}$），比较平坦轨迹训练与基于原始动作分割的训练方式；使用LoRA微调方案和固定超参数，在6个未见任务上评估m={0,1,3,5,10}个示范下的少样本迁移性能，并在LIBERO-Long上验证泛化性；进一步进行隐藏状态子空间消融实验以验证因果关系。

Result: 原始动作训练模型仅用3个示范即达到微调上限性能的78%，而平坦训练需10个示范，显示3倍样本效率优势；该结果在不同种子、架构和数据集中一致；消融实验证明原始解码子空间对迁移性能有因果必要性（下降32个百分点）；同时发现并纠正了分块策略评估中的方法论偏差。

Conclusion: 原始动作感知训练能有效构建可组合的子技能库，显著提升VLA模型在工业场景下的少样本任务迁移能力，且该表示具有因果作用；研究还揭示了评估chunked策略时的统计偏差问题，为未来评估提供警示。

Abstract: Deploying vision-language-action (VLA) policies in industrial environments requires the ability to teach new tasks at low cost, a property current VLAs lack, since each new task requires fine-tuning. We investigate whether primitive-aware training produces a transferable artifact: a learned library of sub-skills that can be composed at inference time, conditioned on a small number of demonstrations, to perform tasks the policy was never trained on. We train two VLA architectures with different inductive biases, OpenVLA and $π_{0.5}$, on the REASSEMBLE contact-rich assembly dataset under matched LoRA fine-tuning recipes and locked hyperparameters, varying training between flat trajectories and primitive-segmented episodes with primitive-specific language prompts. We hold out 6 object-task combinations from training and evaluate few-shot transfer: models receive $m \in \{0, 1, 3, 5, 10\}$ demonstrations of a held-out task and attempt execution without weight updates. We replicate across three training seeds and validate on a second dataset (LIBERO-Long). Primitive-trained models reach 78% of fine-tuned upper-bound performance with only m=3 demonstrations, while flat-trained models require m=10 demonstrations to reach the same level -- a $3\times$ sample efficiency gap that replicates across seeds, architectures, and datasets. To establish causation, we ablate the primitive-decodable subspace of hidden states and show few-shot transfer degrades by 32 percentage points while ablating a random subspace of equal dimensionality has no effect, indicating primitive representations are causally necessary rather than incidentally correlated with transfer. We identify and correct a methodological pitfall in evaluating chunked policies: family-wise inflation of single-step action-range gates produces order-of-magnitude higher false-failure rates against ground-truth human demonstrations.

</details>


### [133] [Geometry-Aware Control Barrier Functions for Collision Avoidance via Bernstein Polynomial Approximations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30696)
*Siwon Jo,Yanze Zhang,Yupeng Yang,Wenhao Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于Bernstein-Polynomial Signed Distance Fields（BP-SDFs）的新型几何感知控制屏障函数（CBF），用于解决具有不规则几何形状的机器人和障碍物在导航中的安全避障问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法在处理不规则几何形状时过于保守或需要大量局部基元，导致约束数量增加并影响实时性能，因此需要一种更高效且精确的安全导航方法。

Method: 利用Bernstein多项式的可微性构建Signed Distance Fields（SDFs），提出一种统一表示机器人与障碍物的几何感知CBF，并通过闭环控制施加控制约束。

Result: 在多种环境下的仿真中验证了该方法在单机器人导航和异构多机器人避碰中的安全性和实时性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的BP-SDFs-CBF方法能够有效减少保守性，降低约束复杂度，提升复杂环境中安全导航的效率与性能。

Abstract: Safe navigation often relies on well-defined conditions based on the shape of robots and obstacles, and can be challenging when they have irregular geometries. While Control Barrier Functions (CBFs) offer an efficient mechanism to enforce safe set forward invariance, common shape surrogates (e.g., spheres or super-ellipsoids) either are overly conservative in unstructured scenes or require many local primitives, which inflates constraint counts and degrades real-time performance. In this paper, we introduce a novel geometry-aware Control Barrier Function (CBF) based on Bernstein-Polynomial Signed Distance Fields (BP-SDFs). It provides a unified way to represent the obstacles and robots, so as to represent the barrier function with a unified minimum distance. Benefiting from the differentiability of the Bernstein polynomials, one can easily enforce the control constraints in a closed loop. We validate the method's efficiency and performance to guarantee safety in single-robot navigation and heterogeneous multi-robot collision avoidance via simulations under different environments.

</details>


### [134] [GSAM: A Generalizable and Safe Robotic Framework for Articulated Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30740)
*Beichen Shao,Mengying Xie,Heng Su,Wanyi Zhang,Mingyan Li,Yan Ding,Fausto Giunchiglia,Chao Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为GSAM的通用且安全的机器人框架，用于解决铰接物体操作中的泛化性和碰撞问题，通过视觉感知、VLM细化和LLM生成约束，在50个铰接任务上验证了其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理铰接物体操作时忽视了物体多样性及末端执行器与手柄交互的复杂性，导致泛化能力差和易发生破坏性碰撞。

Method: 设计了一个基于视觉的感知器生成运动学参数，并利用微调后的视觉语言模型（VLM）结合思维链（COT）常识推理来优化感知结果；同时构建交互约束函数生成器，结合大语言模型（LLM）将物体、姿态和避障知识转化为轨迹与姿态规划中的功能化约束，并通过运动学感知的操作规划器验证可达性。

Result: 在50个铰接任务（涵盖5类物体和随机初始化的末端-手柄配置）上的实验表明，GSAM相比最优基线标准差降低3.1%，操作成功率提高36.0%。

Conclusion: GSAM在实际场景中展现出更强的物体泛化能力和交互安全性，有效提升了铰接物体操作的鲁棒性与可靠性。

Abstract: Articulated object manipulation is a unique challenge for service robots. Existing methods employ end-to-end policy learning, visionmotion planning, and large-language/visual-language model (LLM/VLM), but often overlook the diversity of articulated objects and the complexity of interactions between end-effector and handle, leading to limited generalization and destructive collisions. To address this, we propose GSAM, a generalizable and safe robotic framework for articulated object manipulation. Specifically, a vision-based perceiver generates the kinematic parameters. Considering that pre-trained markers in perceiver yield raw estimations that may deviate from commonsense, we present a f ine-tuned VLM-based refiner, using chain-of-thought (COT) commonsense reasoning to refine perception. To prevent destructive collisions, we design an interaction constraint function generator, integrating articulated object, interaction pose, and obstacle avoidance knowledge into a base. LLM then functionalize these constraints and apply them to trajectory and posture planning. A kinematic-aware manipulation planner verifies reachability for trajectory and posture. Experiments on 50 hinge tasks across 5 object categories and 50 randomly initialized end-effectorhandle configurations show that GSAM reduces standard deviation by 3.1% and improves manipulation success rate by 36.0% compared to the best baseline, respectively demonstrating the superior object generalization and interaction safety of GSAM in practical scenarios.

</details>


### [135] [SSR: Scaling Surefooted and Symmetric Humanoid Traversal to the Open World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30770)
*Ruiqi Yu,Yiwen Wang,Yuan Hao,Jun WU,Qiuguo Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为SSR的端到端框架，用于基于视觉的人形机器人在开放世界中的稳定行走，通过想象落脚点引导、对称性增强和场景特定运动先验实现安全、自然的全身体行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现人形机器人在复杂、动态真实环境中安全可靠的足部放置与协调运动是实际部署的关键挑战。

Method: 提出SSR框架，引入想象落脚点引导以预测并优化摆动脚步的接触稳定性，采用等变潜空间对称增强来提升双侧协调性，并利用地形特定的多判别器运动先验促进类人行为生成。

Result: 实验表明，SSR能在多种真实地形（如不同结构的楼梯、大间隙和高平台）上实现安全稳定的高质量运动，并支持户外开放环境中的长距离可靠行走。

Conclusion: SSR通过联合学习视觉感知、落脚点评估与全身协调控制，有效提升了人形机器人在开放世界复杂地形中的行走能力。

Abstract: Extending humanoid traversal to the open world is key to practical deployment in human environments, but remains challenging. The robot must use vision to ensure safe and reliable foot placement on heterogeneous terrain under highly dynamic motion, while producing coordinated, natural whole-body behaviors. We propose SSR, an efficient end-to-end framework for egocentric vision-based humanoid traversal that jointly learns these capabilities. SSR introduces imagined foothold guidance, which learns to model forthcoming swing-foot contacts and evaluates their support to guide pre-touchdown swings toward stable regions, reducing edge slips. It further employs equivariant latent-space symmetry augmentation to efficiently induce bilateral coordination under high-dimensional visual observations, and uses terrain-specific multi-discriminator motion priors to encourage human-like behavior across scenes. Extensive experiments show that SSR achieves safe, stable, and high-quality locomotion on diverse real-world terrains, including stairs with varied structures and extreme challenges such as wide gaps and high platforms, while enabling reliable long-horizon traversal in open outdoor environments.

</details>


### [136] [Object-Informed Model Predictive Path Integral Control for Non-Prehensile Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30778)
*Nikola Raicevic,Bharath Raam Radhakrishnan,Chenbin Yu,Ki Myung Brian Lee,Nikolay Atanasov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种分层的模型预测路径积分（MPPI）控制方法，通过物体级规划引导机器人级规划，提升非抓取操作中的长视距规划效率和任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非抓取操作中由于欠驱动和不连续交互，长视距规划具有挑战性，传统方法难以高效处理。

Method: 采用分层MPPI控制框架：首先求解简化的仅物体动力学问题得到物体轨迹，再将其作为参考用于联合的机器人-物体规划。

Result: 在仿真中任务成功率提高40%，控制频率快26%；在真实实验中成功率提高20%，计算开销与常规MPPI相当。

Conclusion: 该方法通过引入物体级先验信息显著提升了长视距非抓取操作的规划效率与鲁棒性，适用于复杂避障推送任务。

Abstract: Long-horizon planning for non-prehensile robot manipulation is challenging due to underactuated and discontinuous interactions. We propose a hierarchical formulation of model predictive path integral (MPPI) control that guides robot-level planning with a separately computed object-level plan to achieve efficient long-horizon prediction. We first solve a simplified object-only problem, assuming the object can be actuated directly, and use the planned object trajectory as a reference in solving the joint robot-object planning problem. We evaluate our method in both simulation and hardware using a 6-DoF xArm6 manipulator to perform object pushing tasks in which the target object must reach a goal while avoiding static obstacles, necessitating non-myopic reasoning. Our object-informed MPPI increases task success by 40\% with a 26\% faster control frequency in simulation, and by 20\% in real experiments with similar computation as regular MPPI.

</details>


### [137] [Two Degree-of-Freedom Vibratory Transport in a Grasp](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30780)
*C. L. Yako,Shenli Yuan,Kenneth Salisbury*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用非对称振动实现夹持部件二维自由度（2-DoF）手内操作的方法，通过闭环位置控制产生周期性黏滑波形，并理论与实验验证了波形参数对部件平均速度的影响，同时开发了可实现平移和旋转的2-DoF振动表面，实现了多种部件的双向平移与旋转操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了实现对夹持部件的高效、可控的二维自由度手内操作，克服传统方法在微小或复杂运动控制上的局限性。

Method: 采用闭环位置控制生成非对称黏滑振动波形，分析粘滞加速度和滑动加速度两个参数对部件运动的影响，并构建实验平台结合高分辨率编码器记录部件运动，验证理论模型；设计具有平移和旋转功能的2-DoF振动表面，并集成于平行钳爪中进行实验验证。

Result: 理论分析揭示了波形参数与平均速度之间的关系，实验结果验证了该趋势；成功实现了多种被夹持部件的双向平移与绕法向旋转，并证实平移中的波形参数规律同样适用于平面内旋转。

Conclusion: 非对称振动结合闭环控制可用于实现精确的2-DoF手内操作，所提出的波形参数调控策略在平移和旋转运动中均有效，为无外部辅助的手内操作提供了可行方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we use asymmetric vibrations to demonstrate two degree-of-freedom (DoF) in-hand manipulation of grasped parts. The asymmetric vibrations are achieved through closed-loop position control of a moving surface, which applies a periodic stick-slip waveform to the part to be manipulated. We show analytically how two vibratory waveform parameters, the sticking acceleration and the slipping acceleration, affect average part velocity when moving against gravity. The theoretical trends are then validated using an experimental setup where the squeeze force is controlled and part motion is recorded by a high-resolution encoder. We also develop a 2-DoF vibratory surface capable of translation in one direction and rotation about the surface normal. Using two of these 2-DoF surfaces in a parallel jaw gripper configuration, we bidirectionally translate and rotate a variety of grasped parts, as well as demonstrate that the same waveform trends for translation also persist for in-plane rotation.

</details>


### [138] [Feat2Go: Visual Feature-Grounded Value Estimation for Embodied Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30795)
*Junyang Shu,Zhiwei Lin,Bingqing Wei,Yongtao Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Feat2Go的细粒度价值评估框架，用于具身强化学习中的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型，通过利用预训练的视觉世界模型生成连续进度目标，并据此重塑终端奖励，从而在无需人工设计奖励的情况下提升策略性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLA模型在强化学习中面临稀疏监督和长视野操作中难以设计有效奖励信号的问题，限制了其性能提升。

Method: Feat2Go首先利用预训练的视觉世界模型，通过测量补丁级与子目标状态的相似性，并结合基于趋势的聚类将 episode 划分为语义阶段，生成连续进度目标；然后训练一个具身价值模型来预测当前观测和任务指令下的结构进展，并用该预测值进行奖励塑形，优化策略。

Result: 在ManiSkill3和RoboTwin 2.0上的实验表明，Feat2Go显著提升了现有VLA模型的性能：在ManiSkill3上，OpenVLAOFT的平均OOD成功率从17.5%提升至82.9%，ID性能保持在96.9%；在RoboTwin 2.0的域随机化任务中达到88.8%的平均成功率，优于先前方法。

Conclusion: Feat2Go是一种兼容现有VLA强化学习流程的有效框架，无需手动奖励工程即可通过细粒度价值估计显著提升模型在单臂和双臂操作任务中的表现。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning is a promising approach for improving the capabilities of vision-language-action (VLA) models while avoiding the heavy data requirements of imitation learning. However, its effectiveness for VLA models is often constrained by sparse supervision and the difficulty of designing informative reward signals for long-horizon manipulation. In this work, we present Feat2Go, a fine-grained value estimation framework for embodied reinforcement learning. Specifically, Feat2Go first derives a continuous progress target from a pretrained visual world model by measuring patch-level similarity to subgoal states and partitioning episodes into semantic stages with trend-based clustering. We then train an embodied value model to predict this structural progress from the current observation and task instruction, and use the predicted value to reshape terminal rewards during policy optimization. The proposed framework is compatible with existing VLA policy reinforcement learning pipelines, including PPO and GRPO, and does not rely on manual reward engineering. Extensive experiments on ManiSkill3 and RoboTwin 2.0 demonstrate that Feat2Go consistently improves the performance of existing VLA models under both single-arm and bimanual manipulation settings. More specifically, on ManiSkill3, Feat2Go improves OpenVLAOFT from 17.5% to 82.9% average out-of-distribution success while retaining 96.9% in-distribution performance. On RoboTwin 2.0, Feat2Go achieves an average success rate of 88.8% in domain-randomized task settings, outperforming prior reinforcement learning methods.

</details>


### [139] [Hide-and-Seek in Trajectories: Discovering Failure Signals for VLA Runtime Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30834)
*Seongheon Park,Wendi Li,Changdae Oh,Samuel Yeh,Zsolt Kira,Michael Hagenow,Sharon Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Hide-and-Seek的VLA失败检测框架，通过粗粒度监督学习结合对比目标，在无步级标注的情况下实现对失败动作的定位和时序结构化信号的生成，显著提升了多任务失败检测性能并具有良好的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLA模型在实际部署中容易出现执行失败，而当前的失败检测方法依赖昂贵的重采样、外部模型或无法精确定位局部失败信号，因此需要一种高效且精准的失败检测机制。

Method: 将VLA失败检测建模为粗粒度监督学习问题，结合跨轨迹和轨迹内对比学习目标，仅利用轨迹级标签来诱导出时间结构化的失败信号，并定位指示失败的动作。

Result: 在LIBERO、VLABench和真实机器人平台上对OpenVLA、$π_0$和$π_{0.5}$三种VLA策略进行了评估，结果显示该方法在符合性预测下实现了最先进的多任务失败检测性能，并具备良好的准确率-及时性权衡。

Conclusion: Hide-and-Seek能够在无需步级标注的情况下有效检测VLA模型的执行失败，具有良好的实用性、泛化能力和实际部署潜力。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models enable robots to follow natural language instructions and generalize across diverse tasks, but they remain vulnerable to execution failures that compromise reliability in real-world deployment. Detecting such failures during execution is therefore critical for the robust deployment of embodied systems. Existing failure detection methods either rely on expensive action resampling or external models, while alternatives propagate trajectory-level labels uniformly across every timestep, obscuring localized failure signals. In this paper, we propose \textbf{Hide-and-Seek}, a framework that formulates VLA failure detection as a coarsely supervised learning problem. By combining inter-trajectory and intra-trajectory contrastive objectives, Hide-and-Seek localizes failure-indicative actions and induces temporally structured failure signals from trajectory-level supervision alone, without any step-level annotation. We evaluate Hide-and-Seek on LIBERO, VLABench, and a real-world robotic platform across three representative VLA policies: OpenVLA, $π_0$, and $π_{0.5}$.Our method achieves state-of-the-art multi-task failure detection performance with a practical accuracy--timeliness trade-off under conformal prediction, and generalizes well to both seen and unseen tasks.

</details>


### [140] [High-Load-Density Electro-Permanent Magnetic Foot with Controllable Adhesion for Quadruped Wall-Climbing Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30849)
*An Li,Bo Tao,I-Ming Chen,Han Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于四足机器人在铁磁表面可靠攀爬的高负载密度电磁足，具备可控粘附力，采用力反馈环形Halbach阵列永磁体（CHN-EPM）粘附单元和磁化控制系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了实现四足机器人在复杂铁磁表面上（如天花板、垂直墙、曲面等）稳定可靠的攀爬运动，需要开发具有高负载、强适应性和可控粘附性能的足端装置。

Method: 设计了具有三维磁路结构和聚磁效应的环形Halbach阵列永磁体（CHN-EPM），结合磁化驱动器与两阶段脉冲电流控制策略调节激励电流幅值与持续时间，并集成柔性压力传感器实现接触力反馈，以精确控制磁化状态和粘附切换。

Result: CHN-EPM实现了超过1000 N的最大粘附力，负载重量比超过200:1，对气隙变化不敏感，在部分接触条件下仍能维持有效粘附；系统可准确可靠地控制磁化状态，并实现在涂漆、穿孔和弯曲铁磁表面的稳定攀爬。

Conclusion: 所提出的高负载密度电磁足具备优异的粘附性能和环境适应性，成功集成于Unitree GO2四足机器人，验证了其在多种复杂铁磁表面上的稳定攀爬能力，为高空作业机器人提供了可行的技术方案。

Abstract: To enable reliable climbing locomotion of quadruped robots on ferromagnetic surfaces, this paper presents a high-load-density electro-permanent magnetic foot with controllable adhesion, featuring force-feedback circular Halbach-net electro-permanent magnet (CHN-EPM) adhesion units and a magnetization control system. Due to its three-dimensional magnetic circuit structure and flux-concentration effect, the CHN-EPM enables a distributed parallel magnetic flux path with enhanced flux utilization, resulting in reduced sensitivity to air-gap variations and allowing effective adhesion to be maintained even under partial contact conditions. The proposed CHN-EPM generates a maximum adhesion force exceeding 1000 N with a load-to-weight ratio over 200:1. A magnetization driver and a two-stage pulse current control strategy are developed to regulate the excitation current amplitude and duration, enabling accurate and reliable magnetization. By incorporating a flexible pressure sensor for contact force feedback, the system can effectively monitor attachment and detachment states, ensuring robust adhesion switching under uncertain contact conditions. The proposed system is integrated into a commercial quadruped robot (Unitree GO2), demonstrating high-load adhesion on ceiling and vertical-wall surfaces and stable locomotion on painted, perforated, and curved ferromagnetic surfaces.

</details>


### [141] [Trajectory Planning for Non-Communicating Mobile Robots using Inverse Optimal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30906)
*Nina Majer,Yannick Epple,Xin Ye,Stefan Schwab,Sören Hohmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合轨迹规划与预测的算法，通过逆最优控制估计机器人的目标状态，并考虑自预测与其他机器人视角进行联合预测，从而实现更高效的避碰交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在非通信移动机器人之间实现高效的避碰交互，需要准确预测其他机器人的意图并进行合理的轨迹规划。

Method: 采用逆最优控制估计未知的目标状态，并结合自我预测与其他机器人视角解决联合预测问题，将预测结果用于轨迹规划。

Result: 在2-8个机器人的仿真中，相比基于恒定加速度估计的方法，任务完成时间中位数加快了9.8%，且求解器始终能找到可行解。

Conclusion: 所提方法在提高多机器人系统避碰效率和规划可靠性方面具有显著优势。

Abstract: To enable an efficient interaction of non-communicating mobile robots in collision avoidance scenarios, we present a novel combined trajectory planning and prediction algorithm. Inverse optimal control is used to estimate unknown goal states of all robots based on observed past trajectories. Each robot also takes the perspective of other robots in considering self-prediction and solves a joint prediction problem using the estimated goal states. The resulting predictions are then considered for planning. Simulation results of scenarios with 2-8 robots show that the median of the durations until all vehicles reach their goals is 9.8 % faster compared to planning with constant acceleration based estimated goal states. Moreover, the proposed approach never leads to the solver being unable to find a solution to the planning or prediction problem.

</details>


### [142] [RDGen: Demonstration Generation for High-Quality Robot Learning via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30957)
*Zijian Zhu,Menglin Zou,Zhuang Li,Yaojie Tu,Xinhai Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为RDGen的仿真到现实的强化学习框架，用于生成高质量的机器人演示轨迹，以替代昂贵且难以扩展的人类遥操作数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型受限于高质量机器人轨迹数据的获取，而人类遥操作收集数据成本高、效率低，难以规模化。

Method: RDGen结合了基于视觉语言模型的任务解析器、Grounding DINO物体定位器和从仿真迁移到真实机器人的强化学习策略，利用成功的RL rollout生成清洁的高质量演示数据，并在仿真中大规模生成额外轨迹。

Result: 在抓取放置任务实验中，迁移后的RL策略实现了高任务成功率，相比人类遥操作，RDGen生成的轨迹更平滑，下游VLA模型性能更优。

Conclusion: 强化学习生成的演示可以作为更可靠、一致的监督信号，用于机器人策略学习，具备替代人类演示的潜力。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have emerged as a promising paradigm for general-purpose robot control. However, their performance remains fundamentally constrained by the availability of high-quality robot trajectory data. In current robot learning practice, such data are primarily collected through human teleoperation, which is labor-intensive, costly, and difficult to scale. In this paper, we propose RDGen, a sim-to-real reinforcement learning framework for generating high-quality robot demonstrations. Rather than employing reinforcement learning solely as the final control policy, RDGen leverages trained RL policies as a structured trajectory generator. The system consists of a VLM-based task parser that identifies task-relevant objects, a Grounding DINO-based object localizer, and an RL policy transferred from simulation to the real robot. Successful rollouts are then harvested as clean, high-quality demonstrations for downstream VLA training, while the simulation stage further provides a scalable source of additional trajectories at little marginal cost. Experiments on a pick-and-place task demonstrate that the transferred RL policy achieves a high task success rate. Compared with human teleoperation, RDGen produces significantly smoother trajectories and yields superior downstream VLA performance. These results indicate that RL-generated demonstrations can serve as more reliable and consistent supervisory signals for robot policy learning.

</details>


### [143] [A study on a Real-Time VR-Based Teleoperation Framework for Manipulator in Dynamic Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30989)
*InGyu Choi,GeonYeong Go,SunWoo Ahn,HyoJae Kang,Min-Sung Kang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种支持实时操作和避障的VR遥操作系统框架，结合GPU加速的逆运动学与轨迹优化，在7自由度机械臂上验证了其在静态和动态障碍物环境下的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VR遥操作研究多用于数据采集，缺乏对动态障碍、工作空间变化和碰撞风险的处理能力，难以保障操作安全。为实现实际部署中的安全性与鲁棒性，需要能够低延迟响应动态环境并容错的系统。

Method: 设计了一个集成GPU加速逆运动学和轨迹优化的VR遥操作框架，在每个控制周期内根据机器人约束生成可行的关节指令，并在VR界面中实现实时操控与碰撞感知运动生成。

Result: 在三种场景（无障碍、静态障碍、移动障碍）下对7-DoF机械臂进行实验，系统表现出稳定的在线行为，能根据操作者指令生成运动路径，并在遇到障碍时自动产生安全绕行路径。

Conclusion: 该框架有效实现了兼顾操作意图保持与动态避障的实时遥操作，提升了在复杂动态环境下的安全性与可用性。

Abstract: Robot teleoperation enables safe, non-contact task execution in hazardous environments where direct human access is difficult, and its application has expanded with recent VR technologies. Many VR teleoperation studies, however, have primarily served as data-collection tools for robot imitation learning, so they often do not explicitly address dynamic obstacles, workspace changes, or collision risks during operation. For real deployment aimed at operator safety, teleoperation must react to dynamic situations with low latency and remain robust to mistakes made by inexperienced operators. This paper presents a VR teleoperation framework that supports real-time manipulation while handling collisions with both static and moving obstacles. The framework integrates GPU-accelerated inverse kinematics and trajectory optimization within a VR interface to generate feasible joint commands at each control cycle under robot constraints. Experiments with a 7-DoF manipulator demonstrate stable online behavior and collision-aware motion generation across three scenarios: obstacle-free, static-obstacle, and moving-obstacle environments. The results indicate that the proposed approach generates motion consistent with the operator's command while producing safe detours when obstacles interfere with the commanded path.

</details>


### [144] [Can Aerial VLA Models Cooperate? Evaluating Closed-Loop Air-Ground Coordination with CARLA-Air](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31066)
*Tianle Zeng,Yanci Wen,Xueang Yu,Hong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了空中视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在空地协同任务中的表现，提出了CARLA-Air仿真环境以实现无人机（UAV）与地面车（UGV）的物理一致交互，并通过实验发现当前VLA模型难以实现稳定协作，指出零样本下的空地协同需要伙伴状态 grounding、低延迟协调和团队目标对齐三个关键要素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 aerial VLA 模型在单无人机任务中表现良好，但其是否能扩展到无人机与地面车的协同作业尚不明确，尤其是在共享闭环物理环境中需精确协调的情况下。

Method: 构建了统一的仿真环境 CARLA-Air，集成 CARLA 和 AirSim 于同一 Unreal Engine 运行时，实现共享世界状态、物理步进和感知流程，用于评估典型 aerial VLA 模型在移动平台降落和遮挡恢复护送两项任务中的空地协作能力。

Result: 实验表明当前 aerial VLA 模型虽能跟踪或跟随地面伙伴，但难以形成稳定的协作行为；状态提示效果有限，简单的双向交互无法持续提升性能且可能放大错误。

Conclusion: 在文本指令接口下，实现零样本空地协同 VLA 需要突破当前范式，引入显式的伙伴状态理解、低延迟动作协调以及团队级目标对齐机制。

Abstract: Recent aerial vision-language-action (VLA) models show promising single-UAV capabilities, such as tracking moving objects and navigating to language-specified landmarks. However, it remains unclear whether these capabilities can transfer to air-ground cooperation, where a UAV and a UGV must act jointly in a shared, closed-loop physical world.
  We study this question with CARLA-Air, a single-process air-ground evaluation environment that unifies CARLA and AirSim inside one Unreal Engine runtime. By sharing the same world state, physics tick, and sensing pipeline, CARLA-Air enables physically consistent UAV--UGV interaction and precise measurement of simulation-timestamp alignment and effective coordination latency.
  Using CARLA-Air, we evaluate representative aerial VLA and planning baselines on two complementary diagnostic tasks: moving-platform landing and occlusion-recovery escort. The results show that current aerial VLA models can often track or follow a ground partner, but struggle to convert this single-agent competence into stable cooperative behavior. State prompting provides limited benefit, and naive bidirectional interaction fails to consistently improve performance and can amplify errors for most baselines. These findings suggest that, under the tested text-based cue interfaces, zero-shot cooperative air-ground VLA requires three components beyond the current paradigm: explicit partner-state grounding, low-latency action coordination, and team-level objective alignment. Our code is available at https://github.com/louiszengCN/CarlaAir.

</details>


### [145] [Seeing Fast and Slow: Bimodal 3D Scene Graphs for Open-set Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31067)
*Marcel Bartholomeus Prasetyo,Shrutika Vishal Thengane,A Manicka Praveen,Yi Loo,Malika Meghjani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于开放集任务的双模态3D场景图生成方法BiMoSG，能够在“快速”模式下生成粗略场景图，并在需要时切换到“慢速”模式以生成更精细的开放词汇3D场景图，显著提高了效率并支持实时任务执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在开放集任务中根据环境上下文和信息变化灵活切换粗略与精细的场景表示，提升机器人在探索过程中对任务相关物体的识别效率。

Method: 提出BiMoSG方法，结合“快速”模式生成粗略3D场景图，默认使用该模式；当检测到可能包含任务相关物体的区域时，切换到“慢速”模式生成更精细的开放词汇3D场景图。

Result: BiMoSG比现有的开源最先进方法显著更快，能够将场景图生成与任务执行集成，实现实时部署。

Conclusion: BiMoSG通过双模态机制有效平衡了效率与精度，适用于开放集任务中的实时场景理解与机器人执行。

Abstract: Open-set task execution can significantly benefit from seamlessly switching between coarse and fine scene representations depending on the context and the evolving information as the robot explores the environment. For example, it is often sufficient to start with a coarse scene representation initially and only employ a finer, more granular scene representation when the robot encounters regions which are likely to contain the task relevant objects. Hence, in this work, we propose BiMoSG, a bimodal 3D scene graph generation approach for open-set tasks. BiMoSG employs a "fast" mode by default to efficiently generate a coarse 3D scene graph and can switch to a "slow" mode for generating a finer open vocabulary 3D scene graph of task relevant objects. We demonstrate that our proposed 3D scene graph generation approach is significantly faster than the open-source state-of-the-art approaches. This allows us to integrate the scene graph generation process with task execution for real-time deployment.

</details>


### [146] [Don't Fool Me Twice: Adapting to Adversity in the Wild with Experience-Driven Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31119)
*Navin Sriram Ravie,Andrew Jong,Krrish Jain,John Liu,Omar Alama,Bijo Sebastian,Sebastian Scherer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为“Don't Fool Me Twice”的持续学习框架，使移动机器人能通过语义归因在线学习并应对未知环境中的动态干扰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在未见过的非结构化环境中，难以预知所有针对特定机器人的危险，现有方法难以捕捉具身交互相关的动态 adversity。

Method: 框架结合视觉语言模型（VLM）预测干扰的可能成因，利用核回归建模局部扰动，并通过语义体素模型估计认知不确定性，实现对异常行为的语义归因与持续学习。

Result: 在仿真和真实硬件上验证了四种假设，跨不同机器人形态和 adversity 模式均表现出有效的在线学习与恢复能力。

Conclusion: 该框架使机器人能够从经验中持续学习动态干扰的根源，提升在开放世界中的适应性、预测与规划能力。

Abstract: In robotics, dangers and adversity modes are often embodiment-specific and relative to each agent. A frontier of autonomous mobile robotics is to enable agents to operate effectively in the wild in unseen unstructured environments. A significant challenge in unseen unstructured environments is that it may not be possible to predict all the dangers to the specific robot. Although recent work has used large foundation vision-language models (VLMs) to preemptively predict an exhaustive list of common-sense dangers, it remains difficult to capture possible interaction and embodiment-dependent adversities. We propose a continual learning framework for a mobile embodied agent to learn online from disturbances and attribute anomalous behaviours to causes through semantics, enabling better prediction and planning of the world in the future. Our framework, "Don't Fool Me Twice", first observes disturbances and describes their effects on the robot; this description is augmented with visual context to query a VLM to predict possible causes; the local disturbance is characterized using kernel regression, which allows for efficient, few-shot modeling of transient anomalies. We leverage semantic voxel-centric modeling to estimate epistemic uncertainty, enabling richer downstream recovery by treating interaction-driven disturbances as learnable spatial behaviors. We present four hypotheses and validate them in simulation and on hardware across embodiments and adversity modes.

</details>


### [147] [HARP-VLA: Human-Robot Aligned Representation Learning for Vision-Language-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31234)
*Xiang Zhu,Puzhen Yuan,Yichen Liu,Jianyu Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出HARP框架，通过跨具身对齐的表示学习，利用配对和非配对的人类与机器人视频数据，提升视觉-语言-动作模型在人类视频上的预训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于人类与机器人在视觉观察和可执行动作上存在差异，直接利用人类视频训练机器人策略效果受限，需解决跨具身表征不一致问题。

Method: HARP利用少量配对的人类-机器人演示作为桥梁，结合大量未配对的人类和机器人视频，通过操作中心的辅助任务和源相对的对判别对齐损失，训练机器人适配的视觉编码器和潜在动作模型。

Result: 实验显示HARP改善了人类与机器人之间的表征对齐，在模拟和真实世界操作任务中提升了策略性能，CALVIN任务上平均长度达4.481，真实场景成功率比最强基线提高7.1%。

Conclusion: HARP实现了更有效的视觉与动作表示对齐，支持跨具身的视觉-语言-动作联合训练，显著提升从人类视频中预训练机器人策略的泛化能力。

Abstract: Learning generalizable vision-language-action (VLA) models from large-scale human videos is promising but challenging due to cross-embodiment discrepancies in both visual observations and executable actions. While latent action models reduce the action execution gap by learning action abstractions, they still rely on visual features. Thus, misaligned human and robot visual representations can lead to inconsistencies in policy inputs and induce domain-dependent latent actions, hindering effective co-training with human videos. To address this, we propose HARP, a human-robot aligned representation learning framework for more effective VLA pretraining from human videos. Specifically, HARP uses limited paired human-robot demonstrations as cross-embodiment bridges and abundant unpaired human and robot videos as a scalable dynamics supervision data source. It trains a robot-adapted visual encoder and a latent action model with manipulation-centric auxiliary cues and a source-relative pair-discriminative alignment loss, which adapts robot representations toward human semantics while preserving pair-level discrimination. The learned aligned vision encoder and latent action model provide a unified vision and action representation for VLA-style policy learning, where human and robot videos provide vision-language-to-latent-action supervision and a lightweight robot action head grounds latent actions into executable commands. Experiments on feature visualization, simulation, and realworld manipulation show improved human-robot alignment and downstream policy performance, achieving 4.481 average length on CALVIN ABC$\rightarrow$D and a 7.1\% realworld success rate gain over the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [148] [Before Parc Fermé: RL-Time Pruning for Efficient Embodied LLMs in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31256)
*Luca Benfenati,Ali Azimi,Matteo Risso,Fabio Carapellese,Daniele Jahier Pagliari,Alessio Burrello*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在强化学习（RL）过程中进行剪枝的策略Before Parc Fermé（BPF），用于压缩具身大语言模型（LLM）控制器，相比传统后训练剪枝方法，在任务性能、内存和吞吐量之间实现了更优的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于具身大语言模型在机器人控制中的记忆占用和生成延迟问题，难以实现实时部署；而传统的剪枝时机（如训练后）无法充分利用任务特定监督和闭环反馈信息，因此需要研究更优的剪枝时机。

Method: 提出了两种BPF剪枝变体：BPF-RL在强化学习过程中迭代剪枝；BPF-SFT/RL先在SFT阶段剪枝，再在RL阶段继续剪枝，直至达到目标剪枝比例。实验基于RobotxR1自动驾驶控制 pipeline 和LLM-Pruner框架，与其他剪枝策略对比。

Result: BPF在任务性能与内存、吞吐量之间取得了最佳平衡；BPF-SFT/RL相比直接选用同系列小模型，在参数减少与控制适应性损失的权衡上提升了1.69倍；在Jetson AGX Orin平台上解码吞吐量最高提升27%。

Conclusion: 在训练过程中（尤其是RL阶段）进行剪枝能更好保留关键功能结构，BPF是一种有效的具身LLM控制器压缩策略，有利于实际机器人系统的实时部署。

Abstract: Embodied Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used as reasoning modules in robotic control pipelines to improve human-robot interaction, but their memory and generation latency make real-time deployment difficult. Pruning can reduce these costs, but for controllers that undergo multiple pre- and post-training phases, the crucial question is not only how much to prune, but when pruning should occur.
  In this work, we propose Before Parc Fermé (BPF), a pruning strategy performed during RL that compresses embodied LLM controllers while they are still being optimized for closed-loop behavior. This allows pruning decisions to account for the task-specific supervision and closed-loop feedback that shape the final controller. We propose two variants: BPF-RL, which performs iterative pruning during RL by removing part of the model at predefined training intervals, and BPF-SFT/RL, which first prunes part of the model structure during SFT and then further compresses it during RL using the same iterative strategy as BPF-RL until the target pruning ratio is reached. We evaluate BPF on RobotxR1, an LLM-based autonomous-driving control pipeline, using an established LLM pruning framework (LLM-Pruner), and compare it against post-training pruning, post-training pruning with RL recovery, SFT-stage pruning, and smaller dense models from the same family.
  Our results show that BPF provides the best task-performance vs. memory and throughput trade-off among the considered pruning strategies. When compressing the larger RobotxR1 models, BPF-SFT/RL achieves a $1.69\times$ better size-end-to-end performance trade-off than directly selecting a smaller dense model from the same family, measured as removed parameters per lost percentage point of control adaptability. On the Jetson AGX Orin mounted on the target robotic platform, the compact models improve decode throughput by up to $27\%$.

</details>


### [149] [AR Forcing: Towards Long-Horizon Robot Navigation World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31314)
*Yifei Yang,Zehua Fan,Huan Li,Aoqi Wang,Lida Huang,Haibao Yu,Haiyan Liu,Xuanyao Mao,Jason Bao,Liang Xu,Bingchuan Sun,Yan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为AR Forcing的自回归训练策略，用于解决扩散模型在机器人导航中训练与推理阶段分布不一致的问题，提升了长视野预测的稳定性和轨迹预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在机器人导航中通常使用并行监督训练，但推理时采用自回归方式，导致训练与推理之间存在分布偏移，影响长视野预测的稳定性。

Method: 提出AR Forcing方法，将标准扩散损失融入自回归训练循环中，在每一步使用模型自身的预测更新上下文，并优化单步噪声预测目标，使模型在训练时即接触推理时的状态分布。该方法无需额外模块，保持原有扩散框架不变，易于集成。

Result: 在多个导航数据集（RECON, SCAND, HuRoN, TartanDrive）上的实验表明，AR Forcing相比强基线方法提升了生成图像在长视野导航中的连贯性以及轨迹预测的准确性，增强了模型在复杂已知和未知环境中的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: AR Forcing有效缓解了训练与推理间的分布偏移问题，显著提升了扩散世界模型在长视野机器人导航任务中的性能和稳定性。

Abstract: The diffusion based robot navigation world models are typically trained using parallel supervision, while autoregressive inference is employed during path planning. This results in a distribution shift between training and inference, which destabilizes the performance over long-horizon prediction. We propose AR Forcing, an autoregressive training strategy, which integrates the standard diffusion loss into the autoregressive training loop. At each step, the model uses its own predictions to update the context and optimize the single step noise prediction objective, thereby explicitly exposing the model to the inference state distribution during training. Our method does not require additional discriminators or distribution-matching losses, retains the original diffusion framework and sampler, and is easy to integrate. Experiments on multi-domain navigation datasets (RECON, SCAND, HuRoN, TartanDrive) show that compared with strong baselines, AR Forcing improved the consistency of generated images during long-horizon navigation and the accuracy of predicted trajectories, enhancing robustness of the model in complex known and unknown environments. We will release the code soon.

</details>


### [150] [Surface Constraint Policy for Learning Surface-Constrained and Dynamically Feasible Robot Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31321)
*Shuai Ke,Jiexin Zhang,Huan Zhao,Zhiao Wei,Yikun Guo,Jie Pan,Han Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于扩散模型和表面几何约束编码的表面约束策略（SCP），用于生成满足自由曲面约束的机器人动作，通过结合人类示范和实时视觉观测，在多表面操作任务中实现了更高的任务成功率和接触稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于扩散的模仿学习方法在处理涉及复杂自由曲面约束的任务时存在不足，缺乏对表面几何约束的显式建模且动作生成缺乏动态可行性，导致无法可靠地实现表面对齐和稳定接触。

Method: 首先利用从示范数据中提取的二维加权高斯核函数对表面几何约束进行编码；在此基础上，采用基于扩散的策略从视觉观测和机器人状态反馈等多模态输入中推断任务级动作意图，并通过基于相似性的动作映射方法将这些意图转换为受表面约束的动态运动原语（DMPs）。

Result: 该方法在多个表面操作任务上进行了验证，相比现有技术表现出更高的任务成功率达到和更好的接触稳定性。

Conclusion: 所提出的SCP方法能够有效生成具有结构化几何意图且动力学上可行的动作，显著提升了机器人在复杂自由曲面约束下的操作性能。

Abstract: Diffusion-based imitation learning methods have driven rapid progress in robot dexterous manipulation tasks. However, they have limitations when applied to tasks that involve complex free-form surface constraints because of their lack of explicit surface geometry constraint modeling and the dynamic feasibility issue, resulting in stochastic action generation that fails to achieve reliable surface alignment and maintain stable contact. To address these limitations, we propose a novel surface constraint policy (SCP) for generating robot actions that satisfy free-form surface constraints on the basis of human demonstrations and real-time visual observations. First, the surface geometry constraint is encoded using a two-dimensional weighted Gaussian kernel function that is derived from demonstrations. Building on the encoded surface geometry constraints, the diffusion-based policy is used to infer task-level action intentions from multimodal sensory inputs, including visual observations and robot state feedback. These intentions are further transformed into surface-constrained dynamic movement primitives (DMPs) through a similarity-based action mapping method, thereby enabling smooth and compliant motion execution. The SCP achieves generation of structured surface geometric intent and dynamically admissible actions. The proposed method is validated on multiple surface manipulation tasks and compared with existing techniques. The experimental results demonstrate superior task success rates and contact stability under surface constraints.

</details>
